Test Implementation:
func TestMain_InvalidServeFor_456(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    os.Args = []string{"cmd", "-serve_for", "invalid_service"}
    defer func() { os.Args = os.Args[:1] }() // Reset args after test

    // Capture log output
    logOutput := &bytes.Buffer{}
    log.SetOutput(logOutput)
    defer log.SetOutput(os.Stderr)

    // Act and Assert
    assert.Panics(t, func() { main() }, "Expected main to panic on invalid serve_for value")
    assert.Contains(t, logOutput.String(), "Invalid monitoring service", "Expected log to contain error message")
}

import (
    "bytes"
    "os"
    "testing"
    "time"
    "net/http"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
)

Required Library Installation
go get github.com/stretchr/testify

Test Implementation:
func TestMain_ValidPrometheusServeFor_789(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    os.Args = []string{"cmd", "-serve_for", "prometheus"}
    defer func() { os.Args = os.Args[:1] }() // Reset args after test

    // Capture log output
    logOutput := &bytes.Buffer{}
    log.SetOutput(logOutput)
    defer log.SetOutput(os.Stderr)

    // Mock HTTP server
    go func() {
        http.ListenAndServe(":6222", nil)
    }()
    time.Sleep(1 * time.Second) // Allow server to start

    // Act
    go func() {
        defer func() { recover() }() // Prevent test from exiting due to log.Fatal
        main()
    }()
    time.Sleep(2 * time.Second) // Allow main to execute

    // Assert
    assert.Contains(t, logOutput.String(), "Exporter running at :6222. Server type prometheus", "Expected log to confirm Prometheus service")
}

func TestRegister_DuplicateHandler_003(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    mockHandler := new(MockHandler)
    defer func() {
        handlers = nil // Reset handlers after test
    }()
    Register("testHandler", mockHandler)
    
    defer func() {
        if r := recover(); r == nil {
            t.Errorf("Expected panic when registering duplicate handler")
        }
    }()
    
    // Act
    Register("testHandler", mockHandler)
}

--------------------Test Case--------------------
Import Statements:
import (
  "testing"
  "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
  "github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
  "github.com/stretchr/testify/mock"
)

Test Implementation:
func TestInit_ValidConfig_004(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    mockHandler := new(MockHandler)
    mockHandler.On("Init", mock.Anything).Return(true, nil)
    handlers = map[string]Handler{"testHandler": mockHandler}
    defer func() {
        handlers = nil // Reset handlers after test
    }()
    
    config := `[{"name": "testHandler", "config": {}}]`
    
    // Act
    enabled, err := Init([]byte(config))
    
    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Equal(t, []string{"testHandler"}, enabled)
    mockHandler.AssertExpectations(t)
}

Test Implementation:
func TestPush_ReadyHandlers_006(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    mockHandler := new(MockHandler)
    mockHandler.On("IsReady").Return(true)
    mockPushChannel := make(chan *Receipt, 1)
    mockHandler.On("Push").Return(mockPushChannel)
    handlers = map[string]Handler{"testHandler": mockHandler}
    defer func() {
        handlers = nil // Reset handlers after test
    }()
    
    receipt := &Receipt{}
    
    // Act
    Push(receipt)
    
    // Assert
    require.Len(t, mockPushChannel, 1)
    assert.Equal(t, receipt, <-mockPushChannel)
    mockHandler.AssertExpectations(t)
}

--------------------Test Case--------------------
Import Statements:
import (
    "testing"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
    "net/smtp"
)

Required Library Installation
go get github.com/stretchr/testify

Test Implementation:
func TestValidatorInit_ValidConfig_123(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    validConfig := `{
        "host_url": "https://example.com",
        "languages": ["en", "es"],
        "validation_templ": "{{.subject}}",
        "reset_secret_templ": "{{.body_plain}}",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "login": "smtp-login",
        "sender_password": "smtp-password",
        "auth_mechanism": "plain",
        "max_retries": 5,
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_port": "587",
        "smtp_helo_host": "example.com",
        "insecure_skip_verify": false,
        "domains": ["example.com"],
        "code_length": 6
    }`

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err := validator.Init(validConfig)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Equal(t, "https://example.com", validator.HostUrl)
    assert.Equal(t, []string{"en", "es"}, validator.Languages)
    assert.Equal(t, "sender@example.com", validator.senderEmail)
    assert.Equal(t, smtp.PlainAuth("", "smtp-login", "smtp-password", "smtp.example.com"), validator.auth)
    assert.Equal(t, 5, validator.MaxRetries)
    assert.Equal(t, "587", validator.SMTPPort)
    assert.Equal(t, "example.com", validator.SMTPHeloHost)
    assert.False(t, validator.TLSInsecureSkipVerify)
    assert.Equal(t, []string{"example.com"}, validator.Domains)
    assert.Equal(t, 6, validator.CodeLength)
}

coverage: 12.7% of statements

func TestValidatorInit_MissingFields_789(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    missingFieldsConfig := `{
        "languages": ["en"],
        "validation_templ": "{{.subject}}",
        "reset_secret_templ": "{{.body_plain}}",
        "sender": "sender@example.com"
    }`

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err := validator.Init(missingFieldsConfig)

    // Assert

    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "missing SMTP host")
}

import (
    "testing"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
)

Test Implementation:
func TestValidatorInit_ValidConfig_123(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    validConfig := `{
        "host_url": "https://example.com",
        "languages": ["en", "es"],
        "validation_templ": "{{.subject}}",
        "reset_secret_templ": "{{.body_plain}}",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "login": "smtp-login",
        "sender_password": "smtp-password",
        "auth_mechanism": "plain",
        "max_retries": 5,
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_port": "587",
        "smtp_helo_host": "example.com",
        "insecure_skip_verify": false,
        "domains": ["example.com"],
        "code_length": 6
    }`

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err := validator.Init(validConfig)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Equal(t, "https://example.com", validator.HostUrl)
    assert.Equal(t, []string{"en", "es"}, validator.Languages)
    assert.Equal(t, "sender@example.com", validator.senderEmail)
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.auth)
    assert.Equal(t, 5, validator.MaxRetries)
    assert.Equal(t, "587", validator.SMTPPort)
    assert.Equal(t, "example.com", validator.SMTPHeloHost)
    assert.False(t, validator.TLSInsecureSkipVerify)
    assert.Equal(t, []string{"example.com"}, validator.Domains)
    assert.Equal(t, 6, validator.CodeLength)
}

Test Implementation:
func TestRun_Influx_LowInterval_667(t *testing.T) {
  // Capture log output
  var buf bytes.Buffer
  log.SetOutput(&buf)
  originalFlags := log.Flags()
  log.SetFlags(0) // Disable timestamp etc.
  defer func() {
      log.SetOutput(os.Stderr)
      log.SetFlags(originalFlags)
  }()

  mux := http.NewServeMux()
  args := []string{
      "-serve_for", "influxdb",
      "-influx_organization", "org",
      "-influx_auth_token", "token",
      "-influx_bucket", "bucket",
      "-influx_db_version", "2.0",
      "-influx_push_interval", "5", // Too low
  }
  err := run(args, mux)
  require.NoError(t, err)

  logOutput := buf.String()
  assert.Contains(t, logOutput, "The --influx_push_interval is too low, reset to 10")
  // Check that push routine would start (with adjusted interval)
  assert.Contains(t, logOutput, "InfluxDB push goroutine would start here.")

  // Check root handler setup
  req := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/", nil)
  rr := httptest.NewRecorder()
  mux.ServeHTTP(rr, req)
  assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rr.Code)
  assert.Contains(t, rr.Body.String(), "<p>InfluxDB push path: <a href='/push'>Push</a></p>")

  // Check /push handler setup and simulated success
  reqPush := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/push", nil)
  rrPush := httptest.NewRecorder()
  mux.ServeHTTP(rrPush, reqPush)
  assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rrPush.Code)
  assert.Contains(t, rrPush.Body.String(), "HTTP 200 OK")
  assert.Contains(t, logOutput, "Simulated InfluxDB push successful.")
}

import (
	"bytes"
	"log"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"os"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
)

Test Implementation:
func TestRun_Prometheus_Success_741(t *testing.T) {
  // Capture log output
  var buf bytes.Buffer
  log.SetOutput(&buf)
  originalFlags := log.Flags()
  log.SetFlags(0) // Disable timestamp etc.
  defer func() {
      log.SetOutput(os.Stderr)
      log.SetFlags(originalFlags)
  }()

  mux := http.NewServeMux()
  args := []string{
      "-serve_for", "prometheus",
      "-prom_metrics_path", "/testmetrics",
      "-listen_at", ":7777",
      "-tinode_addr", "http://testhost:1234",
  }
  err := run(args, mux)
  require.NoError(t, err)

  logOutput := buf.String()
  assert.Contains(t, logOutput, "Tinode metrics exporter.")
  assert.Contains(t, logOutput, "Reading Tinode expvar from http://testhost:1234")
  assert.Contains(t, logOutput, "Exporter running at :7777. Server type prometheus")

  // Check root handler setup
  reqRoot := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/", nil)
  rrRoot := httptest.NewRecorder()
  mux.ServeHTTP(rrRoot, reqRoot)
  assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rrRoot.Code)
  assert.Contains(t, rrRoot.Body.String(), "<p>Prometheus exporter path: <a href='/testmetrics'>Metrics</a></p>")

  // Check metrics handler registration (we can't easily test the prometheus handler itself without a running exporter)
  // We check if *some* handler is registered at the correct path.
  // A more robust check would involve inspecting the mux internals or using httptest.Server
  reqMetrics := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/testmetrics", nil)
  rrMetrics := httptest.NewRecorder()
  mux.ServeHTTP(rrMetrics, reqMetrics)
  // Expecting the prometheus handler to write *something* (even if it's an error or empty metrics)
  assert.NotEqual(t, http.StatusNotFound, rrMetrics.Code)
  // Prom handler likely sets content type
  assert.Contains(t, rrMetrics.Header().Get("Content-Type"), "text/plain")
}

Test Implementation:
func TestRun_Influx_Success_PushEnabled_538(t *testing.T) {
  // Capture log output
  var buf bytes.Buffer
  log.SetOutput(&buf)
  originalFlags := log.Flags()
  log.SetFlags(0) // Disable timestamp etc.
  defer func() {
      log.SetOutput(os.Stderr)
      log.SetFlags(originalFlags)
  }()

  mux := http.NewServeMux()
  args := []string{
      "-serve_for", "influxdb",
      "-influx_organization", "org_enabled",
      "-influx_auth_token", "token_enabled",
      "-influx_bucket", "bucket_enabled",
      "-influx_db_version", "2.0",
      "-influx_push_interval", "30", // Enabled
      "-listen_at", ":8888",
      "-tinode_addr", "http://influxhost:5678",
  }
  err := run(args, mux)
  require.NoError(t, err)

  logOutput := buf.String()
  assert.Contains(t, logOutput, "Tinode metrics exporter.")
  assert.Contains(t, logOutput, "Reading Tinode expvar from http://influxhost:5678")
  assert.Contains(t, logOutput, "Exporter running at :8888. Server type influxdb, version 2.0")
  assert.Contains(t, logOutput, "InfluxDB push goroutine would start here.") // Check log indicator

  // Check root handler setup
  reqRoot := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/", nil)
  rrRoot := httptest.NewRecorder()
  mux.ServeHTTP(rrRoot, reqRoot)
  assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rrRoot.Code)
  assert.Contains(t, rrRoot.Body.String(), "<p>InfluxDB push path: <a href='/push'>Push</a></p>")

  // Check /push handler setup and simulated success
  reqPush := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/push", nil)
  rrPush := httptest.NewRecorder()
  mux.ServeHTTP(rrPush, reqPush)
  assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rrPush.Code)
  assert.Contains(t, rrPush.Body.String(), "HTTP 200 OK")
  assert.Contains(t, logOutput, "Simulated InfluxDB push successful.")

  // Check /push handler setup and simulated failure
  reqPushFail := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/push?force_error=true", nil)
  rrPushFail := httptest.NewRecorder()
  mux.ServeHTTP(rrPushFail, reqPushFail)
  assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rrPushFail.Code) // Handler itself returns 200
  assert.Contains(t, rrPushFail.Body.String(), "Simulated Push Error")
  assert.Contains(t, buf.String(), "Simulated InfluxDB push failed.") // Check log

}

// TestPush_HandlerNotReady_222 tests Push when the handler is not ready.
func TestPush_HandlerNotReady_222(t *testing.T) {

    defer resetHandlers()

Import Statements:
import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"sync"
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/mock"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	t "github.com/tinode/chat/server/store/types"
)

Test Implementation:
// TestPush_HandlerReady_ChannelBlocks_444 tests Push when the handler's channel blocks.
func TestPush_HandlerReady_ChannelBlocks_444(t *testing.T) {
    resetHandlers()
    defer resetHandlers()

    // Arrange
    mh := new(mockHandler)
    mh.On("IsReady").Return(true)
    pushChan := make(chan *Receipt) // Unbuffered channel
    mh.On("Push").Return(pushChan)

    Register("testHandler", mh)

    receipt := &Receipt{Channel: "test"}
    var wg sync.WaitGroup
    wg.Add(1)

    // Act
    go func() {
        defer wg.Done()
        Push(receipt) // This will block in the select unless default is hit
    }()

    // Assert
    // We wait briefly to ensure the Push goroutine had a chance to run and hit the select.
    // Since the channel is unbuffered and no one is reading, the send should time out
    // immediately due to the default case.
    wg.Wait() // Ensure the Push call completes

    mh.AssertExpectations(t) // Verify IsReady and Push were called
    // We can't easily assert the default case was taken directly, but we know
    // the function didn't block indefinitely, which implies the default case worked.
    // And the channel should still be empty.
    assert.Len(t, pushChan, 0, "Channel should be empty as the send should have been skipped")
}

    mh.AssertExpectations(t) // Verify that Init was called

    // Calling Push will hit the 'default' case because the unbuffered channel would block.
    Push(receipt)

    // Assert
    mh.AssertExpectations(t) // Verify IsReady and Push were called
    // Assert that nothing was sent to the channel because the default case was taken.
    assert.Len(t, pushChan, 0, "Channel should be empty as the send should have been skipped")
}

    // Assert

    assert.Equal(t, req, sentReq, "Sent request should match the original")

// Mock package-level functions from 'validate' package
var (
    originalResolveTemplatePath = resolveTemplatePath
    originalReadTemplateFile = readTemplateFile
    originalValidateHostURL = validateHostURL
)

// Helper to create a minimal valid template content
const validTemplateContent = `
{{define "subject"}}Subject{{end}}
{{define "body_plain"}}Plain Body {{.Code}}{{end}}
{{define "body_html"}}<p>HTML Body {{.Code}}</p>{{end}}
`

// Helper function to check if a template is valid (contains required parts)
func isTemplateValid(tmpl *textt.Template) error {
    for _, name := range templateParts {
        if tmpl.Lookup(name) == nil {
            return fmt.Errorf("template is missing section %q", name)
        }
    }
    return nil
}

func TestValidatorInit_ValidConfigLoginAuth_391(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_template.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    validConfig := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "https://example.com",
        "languages": ["en", "es"],
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "Test Sender <sender@example.com>",
        "login": "smtp-login",
        "sender_password": "smtp-password",
        "auth_mechanism": "login",
        "max_retries": 5,
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_port": "587",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "insecure_skip_verify": false,
        "domains": ["example.com"],
        "code_length": 8
    }`, validTemplatePath, validTemplatePath) // Use JSON string literal escape

    // Mock functions
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    readTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        // Execute the path template to get the actual path
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        if err != nil {
            return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to execute path template: %w", err)
        }
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()

        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        if err != nil {
            return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock ReadFile failed: %w", err)
        }
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        return tmpl, actualPath, err
    }
    validateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }

    // Restore original functions after test
    t.Cleanup(func() {
        resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
        readTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
        validateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
    })

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(validConfig)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Equal(t, "https://example.com", validator.HostUrl)
    assert.Equal(t, []string{"en", "es"}, validator.Languages)
    assert.Equal(t, validTemplatePath, validator.ValidationTemplFile)
    assert.Equal(t, validTemplatePath, validator.ResetTemplFile)
    assert.Equal(t, "sender@example.com", validator.senderEmail)
    assert.Equal(t, "smtp-login", validator.Login)
    assert.Equal(t, "smtp-password", validator.SenderPassword)
    assert.Equal(t, "login", validator.AuthMechanism)
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.auth)
    _, ok := validator.auth.(*loginAuth) // Check if it's the correct auth type
    assert.True(t, ok, "Expected auth to be of type *loginAuth")
    assert.Equal(t, 5, validator.MaxRetries)
    assert.Equal(t, "smtp.example.com", validator.SMTPAddr)
    assert.Equal(t, "587", validator.SMTPPort)
    assert.Equal(t, "mail.example.com", validator.SMTPHeloHost)
    assert.False(t, validator.TLSInsecureSkipVerify)
    assert.Equal(t, []string{"example.com"}, validator.Domains)
    assert.Equal(t, 8, validator.CodeLength)
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.validationTempl)
    assert.Len(t, validator.validationTempl, 2)
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.resetTempl)
    assert.Len(t, validator.resetTempl, 2)
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.langMatcher)
    assert.Equal(t, "100000000", validator.maxCodeValue.String()) // 10^8
}

func TestValidatorInit_AuthCramMD5_873(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    tempDir := t.TempDir()

        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "login": "smtp-login",
        "sender_password": "smtp-password",
        "auth_mechanism": "cram-md5",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com"
    }`, validTemplatePath, validTemplatePath)

    // Mock functions needed for successful init up to auth mechanism check
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    readTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        content, _ := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        tmpl, _ := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    validateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }
    t.Cleanup(func() {
        resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
        readTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
        validateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
    })


    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(cramMd5Config)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.auth)
    // We can't directly compare the function pointer, but we know it should be non-nil
    // and created by smtp.CRAMMD5Auth. Checking the type isn't straightforward for function types.
    assert.Equal(t, "cram-md5", validator.AuthMechanism)
}

func TestValidatorInit_NoAuth_710(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_template.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    noAuthConfig := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "https://example.com",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com"
    }`, validTemplatePath, validTemplatePath) // login and password omitted

    // Mock functions needed for successful init
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
     readTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        content, _ := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        tmpl, _ := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    validateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }
    t.Cleanup(func() {
        resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
        readTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
        validateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
    })


    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(noAuthConfig)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Nil(t, validator.auth) // No auth should be configured
    assert.Equal(t, "", validator.Login)
}

    validator := &validator{}

func TestValidatorInit_ResolveResetPathError_556(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    config := `{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "login": "login",
        "sender_password": "password",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "validation_templ": "valid_validation",
        "reset_secret_templ": "/invalid/path/reset.tmpl"
    }` // Minimal config

    expectedError := errors.New("cannot resolve reset path")

    // Mock ResolveTemplatePath
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) {
        if path == "/invalid/path/reset.tmpl" {
            return "", expectedError
        }
        // Assume validation path resolves correctly
        if path == "valid_validation" {
            return "valid_validation_resolved", nil
        }
        return path, nil
    }
    t.Cleanup(func() { resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath })

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err := validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError)
}

    // Assert

}

func TestValidatorInit_ReadValidationTemplateError_990(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    // Don't actually create the file to cause a read error
    nonExistentTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "non_existent.tmpl")

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "login": "login",
        "sender_password": "password",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "host_url": "http://host.com",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "languages": ["en"]
    }`, nonExistentTemplatePath, nonExistentTemplatePath)

    expectedError := errors.New("read failed")

    // Mock functions
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    readTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        // Simulate error only for validation template read
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        if actualPath == nonExistentTemplatePath {
             return nil, actualPath, expectedError // Simulate read error
        }
         // Should not be reached for validation, but handle reset case
        content, _ := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        tmpl, _ := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    validateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }
    t.Cleanup(func() {
        resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
        readTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
        validateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
    })

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err := validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError)
}

func TestValidatorInit_InvalidValidationTemplateContent_101(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    invalidTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "invalid_content.tmpl")
    // Missing 'body_html'
    invalidContent := `{{define "subject"}}S{{end}} {{define "body_plain"}}P{{end}}`
    err := os.WriteFile(invalidTemplatePath, []byte(invalidContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    resetTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "reset.tmpl")
    err = os.WriteFile(resetTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)


    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "login": "login",
        "sender_password": "password",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "host_url": "http://host.com",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "languages": ["en"]
    }`, invalidTemplatePath, resetTemplatePath)

    // Mock functions
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    readTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        if err != nil {
            return nil, actualPath, err
        }
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        return tmpl, actualPath, err
    }
    validateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }
    t.Cleanup(func() {
        resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
        readTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
        validateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
    })

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), fmt.Sprintf("parsing %s", invalidTemplatePath))
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), `template is missing section "body_html"`)
}

func TestValidatorInit_ReadResetTemplateError_202(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    nonExistentTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "non_existent_reset.tmpl")

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "login": "login",
        "sender_password": "password",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "host_url": "http://host.com",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "languages": ["en"]
    }`, validTemplatePath, nonExistentTemplatePath)

    expectedError := errors.New("read reset failed")

    // Mock functions
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    readTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
         pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
         if err != nil { return nil, "", err}
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()

        if actualPath == nonExistentTemplatePath {
            return nil, actualPath, expectedError // Simulate read error for reset template

        // Assume validation template reads correctly
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        if err != nil { return nil, actualPath, err}
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        return tmpl, actualPath, err
    }
    validateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }
    t.Cleanup(func() {
        resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
        readTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
        validateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
    })

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError)
}

Required Library Installation

go get github.com/stretchr/testify/require

Test Implementation:


func TestValidatorInit_NoLanguages_404(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "default_template.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "https://example.com",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com"
    }`, validTemplatePath, validTemplatePath) // No "languages" field


    // Mock functions
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    readTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
         // Lang should be empty in this case
         assert.Empty(t, lang)
         pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang}) // lang is ""
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
         if err != nil { return nil, actualPath, err}
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        return tmpl, actualPath, err
    }
    validateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }
    t.Cleanup(func() {
        resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
        readTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
        validateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
    })


    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Nil(t, validator.langMatcher) // No matcher should be created
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.validationTempl)
    assert.Len(t, validator.validationTempl, 1) // Should have one default template
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.resetTempl)
    assert.Len(t, validator.resetTempl, 1)      // Should have one default template
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.validationTempl[0])
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.resetTempl[0])
}

func TestValidatorInit_NoLangReadValidationError_505(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    nonExistentPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "no_such_file.tmpl")

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "https://example.com",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com"
    }`, nonExistentPath, nonExistentPath)

    expectedError := errors.New("read default failed")

    // Mock functions
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    readTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        assert.Empty(t, lang) // Expect empty lang for default
         pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang}) // lang is ""
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        // Fail only on the first call (validation template)
        if actualPath == nonExistentPath {
             return nil, actualPath, expectedError
        }
        // Should not be reached if validation fails
        return nil, actualPath, errors.New("should not reach reset read")
    }
    validateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }
    t.Cleanup(func() {
        resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
        readTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
        validateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
    })

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err := validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError)
}

func TestValidatorInit_NoLangInvalidValidationContent_606(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    invalidTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "invalid_default.tmpl")
    invalidContent := `{{define "subject"}}S{{end}}` // Missing body parts
    err := os.WriteFile(invalidTemplatePath, []byte(invalidContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    resetPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "reset_default.tmpl")
    err = os.WriteFile(resetPath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "https://example.com",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com"
    }`, invalidTemplatePath, resetPath)

    // Mock functions
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    readTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        assert.Empty(t, lang)
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        if err != nil { return nil, actualPath, err}
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        return tmpl, actualPath, err
    }
    validateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }
    t.Cleanup(func() {
        resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
        readTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
        validateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
    })

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), fmt.Sprintf("parsing %s", invalidTemplatePath))
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), `template is missing section "body_plain"`)
}

func TestValidatorInit_NoLangReadResetError_707(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_default.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validPath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    nonExistentResetPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "no_reset.tmpl")

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "https://example.com",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com"
    }`, validPath, nonExistentResetPath)

    expectedError := errors.New("read default reset failed")

    // Mock functions
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    readTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        assert.Empty(t, lang)
         pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        if actualPath == nonExistentResetPath {
             return nil, actualPath, expectedError // Fail on reset read
        }
        // Assume validation reads fine
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
         if err != nil { return nil, actualPath, err}
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        return tmpl, actualPath, err
    }
    validateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }
    t.Cleanup(func() {
        resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
        readTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
        validateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
    })

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError)
}

func TestValidatorInit_ValidateHostURLError_909(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "invalid url spec",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com"
    }`, validTemplatePath, validTemplatePath)

    expectedError := errors.New("invalid host url")

    // Mock functions
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    readTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        content, _ := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        tmpl, _ := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    validateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) {
        // Return error only for the specific input URL
         if origUrl == "invalid url spec" {
             return "", expectedError
         }
         return origUrl, nil // Allow valid URLs if any other test uses this mock inadvertently
    }
    t.Cleanup(func() {
        resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
        readTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
        validateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
    })

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError)
}

func TestValidatorInit_MissingSMTPHost_010(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    // Missing host_url and smtp_helo_host
    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com"
    }`, validTemplatePath, validTemplatePath)

    // Mock functions
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    readTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        content, _ := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        tmpl, _ := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    // Mock ValidateHostURL to return "" for empty input, simulating missing/invalid URL
    validateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) {
         if origUrl == "" { return "", nil}
         // Fail if a non-empty URL is somehow passed
         return "", errors.New("unexpected host url")
    }
    t.Cleanup(func() {
        resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
        readTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
        validateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
    })

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.EqualError(t, err, "missing SMTP host")
}

import (
	"bytes"
	"fmt"
	"math/big"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"
	textt "text/template"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	"github.com/tinode/chat/server/validate"
)

Required Library Installation
go get github.com/stretchr/testify/assert
go get github.com/stretchr/testify/require

Test Implementation:


func TestValidatorInit_DefaultsApplied_111(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    // Minimal config, relying on defaults and host derivation
    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "http://my.cool-chat.app:8080/base/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.provider.net"
    }`, validTemplatePath, validTemplatePath)


    // Mock functions
    resolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    readTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        content, _ := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        tmpl, _ := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    // Use real validateHostURL logic for this test if possible, or mock simply.
    validateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) {
         u, err := url.Parse(origUrl)
         if err != nil || u.Scheme == "" || u.Host == "" {
             return "", fmt.Errorf("invalid URL %s: %w", origUrl, err)
         }
         // Ensure trailing slash
         if origUrl[len(origUrl)-1] != '/' {
             origUrl += "/"
         }
         return origUrl, nil
    }
    t.Cleanup(func() {
        resolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
        readTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
        validateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
    })


    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Equal(t, defaultMaxRetries, validator.MaxRetries) // line 201
    assert.Equal(t, defaultCodeLength, validator.CodeLength) // line 204
    assert.Equal(t, defaultPort, validator.SMTPPort)         // line 209
    assert.Equal(t, "my.cool-chat.app", validator.SMTPHeloHost) // line 194 (derived from HostUrl)
    expectedMaxCode := big.NewInt(0).Exp(big.NewInt(10), big.NewInt(int64(defaultCodeLength)), nil)
    assert.Equal(t, expectedMaxCode, validator.maxCodeValue) // line 206
}

func TestPush_HandlerReady_ChannelBlocks_444(t *testing.T) {
    resetHandlers()
    defer resetHandlers()

    // Arrange
    mh := new(mockHandler)
    mh.On("IsReady").Return(true)
    pushChan := make(chan *Receipt) // Unbuffered channel
    mh.On("Push").Return((chan<- *Receipt)(pushChan))

    Register("testHandler", mh)

    receipt := &Receipt{Channel: "test"}

    // Act
    // Calling Push will hit the 'default' case because the unbuffered channel would block.
    Push(receipt)

    // Assert
    mh.AssertExpectations(t) // Verify IsReady and Push were called
    // Assert that nothing was sent to the channel because the default case was taken.
    select {
    case <-pushChan:
        t.Fatal("Message should not have been sent to the blocking channel")
    default:
        // Expected behavior
    }
}

--------------------Test Case--------------------
Import Statements:
import (
	"bytes"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"math/big"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"
	textt "text/template"
    "strings"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	origvalidate "github.com/tinode/chat/server/validate" // Use alias to avoid conflict
	i18n "golang.org/x/text/language"
    _ "net/smtp" // Import for side effects if needed by auth types, though likely not required here
)

Test Implementation:


// Mock implementation for validate package functions
var (
	mockResolveTemplatePath func(path string) (string, error)
	mockReadTemplateFile    func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error)
	mockValidateHostURL     func(origUrl string) (string, error)
)

// Helper to set up mocks for a test
func setupValidateMocks(t *testing.T) {
	// Store original functions
	originalResolveTemplatePath := origvalidate.ResolveTemplatePath
	originalReadTemplateFile := origvalidate.ReadTemplateFile
	originalValidateHostURL := origvalidate.ValidateHostURL

	// Apply mocks
	origvalidate.ResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) {
		if mockResolveTemplatePath != nil {
			return mockResolveTemplatePath(path)
		}
		return originalResolveTemplatePath(path)
	}
	origvalidate.ReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
		if mockReadTemplateFile != nil {
			return mockReadTemplateFile(pathTempl, lang)
		}
		return originalReadTemplateFile(pathTempl, lang)
	}
	origvalidate.ValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) {
		if mockValidateHostURL != nil {
			return mockValidateHostURL(origUrl)
		}
		return originalValidateHostURL(origUrl)
	}

	// Restore original functions after test
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		origvalidate.ResolveTemplatePath = originalResolveTemplatePath
		origvalidate.ReadTemplateFile = originalReadTemplateFile
		origvalidate.ValidateHostURL = originalValidateHostURL
		// Clear mocks for next test
		mockResolveTemplatePath = nil
		mockReadTemplateFile = nil
		mockValidateHostURL = nil
	})
}

const validTemplateContent = `
{{define "subject"}}Subject {{.Lang}}{{end}}
{{define "body_plain"}}Plain Body {{.Code}} {{.Lang}}{{end}}
{{define "body_html"}}<p>HTML Body {{.Code}} {{.Lang}}</p>{{end}}
`

func TestValidatorInit_ValidConfigLoginAuth_721(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    // Create separate template files for different languages to test path templating
    validTemplatePathEn := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_template_en.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePathEn, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    validTemplatePathEs := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_template_es.tmpl")
    err = os.WriteFile(validTemplatePathEs, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    // Use template syntax in the config path
    validationTemplatePathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_template_{{.lang}}.tmpl")

    validConfig := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "https://example.com/base/",
        "languages": ["en", "es"],
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "Test Sender <sender@example.com>",
        "login": "smtp-login",
        "sender_password": "smtp-password",
        "auth_mechanism": "login",
        "max_retries": 5,
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_port": "587",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "insecure_skip_verify": false,
        "domains": ["example.com"],
        "code_length": 8
    }`, validationTemplatePathTemplate, validationTemplatePathTemplate) // Use same template path for validation and reset for simplicity

    // Mock functions
    mockResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil } // Pass path through
    mockReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        // Execute the path template to get the actual path
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        if err != nil {
            return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to execute path template: %w", err)
        }
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        t.Logf("Mock reading template for lang '%s' from path '%s'", lang, actualPath)

        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        if err != nil {
            return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock ReadFile failed for %s: %w", actualPath, err)
        }
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        if err != nil {
             return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock Parse failed for %s: %w", actualPath, err)
        }
         // Manually check validity here because the real isTemplateValid isn't exported easily for direct call in test mock
        for _, name := range templateParts {
            if tmpl.Lookup(name) == nil {
                 return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock template is missing section %q", name)
            }
        }
        return tmpl, actualPath, err
    }
    mockValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) {
         // Simple validation and ensure trailing slash
         u, err := url.Parse(origUrl)
         if err != nil || u.Scheme == "" || u.Host == "" {
             return "", fmt.Errorf("invalid URL %s: %w", origUrl, err)
         }
         if !strings.HasSuffix(origUrl, "/") {
             origUrl += "/"
         }
         return origUrl, nil
     }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(validConfig)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Equal(t, "https://example.com/base/", validator.HostUrl) // Check trailing slash added by ValidateHostURL mock
    assert.Equal(t, []string{"en", "es"}, validator.Languages)
    assert.Equal(t, validationTemplatePathTemplate, validator.ValidationTemplFile) // Stores the template path
    assert.Equal(t, validationTemplatePathTemplate, validator.ResetTemplFile)
    assert.Equal(t, "sender@example.com", validator.senderEmail)
    assert.Equal(t, "smtp-login", validator.Login)
    assert.Equal(t, "smtp-password", validator.SenderPassword)
    assert.Equal(t, "login", validator.AuthMechanism)
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.auth, "Auth should be configured")
    _, ok := validator.auth.(*loginAuth) // Check if it's the correct auth type
    assert.True(t, ok, "Expected auth to be of type *loginAuth")
    assert.Equal(t, 5, validator.MaxRetries)
    assert.Equal(t, "smtp.example.com", validator.SMTPAddr)
    assert.Equal(t, "587", validator.SMTPPort)
    assert.Equal(t, "mail.example.com", validator.SMTPHeloHost)
    assert.False(t, validator.TLSInsecureSkipVerify)
    assert.Equal(t, []string{"example.com"}, validator.Domains)
    assert.Equal(t, 8, validator.CodeLength)
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.validationTempl)
    assert.Len(t, validator.validationTempl, 2, "Should load templates for 2 languages")
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.resetTempl)
    assert.Len(t, validator.resetTempl, 2, "Should load templates for 2 languages")
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.langMatcher)
    assert.Equal(t, "100000000", validator.maxCodeValue.String()) // 10^8
}

func TestValidatorInit_AuthCramMD5_873(t *testing.T) {

    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    configParts, err := minimalValidConfig(tempDir)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    configParts["login"] = `"smtp-login"`
    configParts["sender_password"] = `"smtp-password"`
    configParts["auth_mechanism"] = `"cram-md5"`
    cramMd5Config := buildConfig(configParts)

    // Mock functions needed for successful init up to auth mechanism check
    mockResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockReadTemplateFile = mockGoodTemplateReader(t, "") // Use default lang ""
    mockValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(cramMd5Config)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.auth)
    // We can't easily check the exact type of smtp.CRAMMD5Auth's result,
    // but we can check that auth is not nil and the mechanism string is correct.
    assert.Equal(t, "cram-md5", validator.AuthMechanism)
    // Check it's not PlainAuth or loginAuth
     _, isPlain := validator.auth.(smtp.Auth) // smtp.PlainAuth returns smtp.Auth
     _, isLogin := validator.auth.(*loginAuth)
     assert.True(t, isPlain || !isLogin, "Auth should be cram-md5, not plain or login")

}

func TestValidatorInit_ResolveResetPathError_556(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir() // Need temp dir for the *valid* validation template
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_validation.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "/invalid/path/reset.tmpl"
    }`, validTemplatePath)

    expectedError := errors.New("cannot resolve reset path")

    // Mock ResolveTemplatePath
    mockResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) {
        if path == "/invalid/path/reset.tmpl" {
            return "", expectedError
        }
        // Assume validation path resolves correctly
        if path == validTemplatePath {
            return validTemplatePath, nil // Return the path itself for simplicity
        }
        return path, nil
    }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError)
}

Test Implementation:
func TestValidatorInit_ReadValidationTemplateError_990(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    // Use a path template that points to a non-existent file
    nonExistentTemplatePathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "non_existent_{{.lang}}.tmpl")
    // Need a valid *reset* template path for the code to reach validation read error
    validResetTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "reset_en.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validResetTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    resetPathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "reset_{{.lang}}.tmpl")


    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "host_url": "http://host.com/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "languages": ["en"]
    }`, nonExistentTemplatePathTemplate, resetPathTemplate)

    expectedError := errors.New("read validation failed")

    // Mock functions
    mockResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        require.NoError(t, err)
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()

        // Simulate error only for validation template read attempt
        if strings.Contains(actualPath, "non_existent") {
             return nil, actualPath, expectedError // Simulate read error
        }
        // Handle reset template read successfully if needed (though should error before)
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        require.NoError(t, err)
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
         require.NoError(t, err)
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    mockValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError) // Comes from validate.ReadTemplateFile(validationPathTempl, lang)
}

    resetTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "reset_en.tmpl") // Valid reset template
    err = os.WriteFile(resetTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    resetPathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "reset_{{.lang}}.tmpl")


    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "host_url": "http://host.com/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "languages": ["en"]
    }`, validationPathTemplate, resetPathTemplate)

    // Mock functions
    mockResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        // Standard mock reader logic, will read invalid or valid content
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        require.NoError(t, err)
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        if err != nil {
            return nil, actualPath, err // Propagate read error if occurs
        }
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
         // Don't require.NoError here, let the main Init function handle parsing error
        return tmpl, actualPath, err // Return parse result (potentially nil template and error)
    }
     mockValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }


    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    // Error comes from isTemplateValid(v.validationTempl[idx])
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), fmt.Sprintf("parsing %s", invalidTemplatePath)) // Path passed to isTemplateValid
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), `template is missing section "body_html"`)
}

Test Implementation:
func TestValidatorInit_ReadResetTemplateError_202(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_en.tmpl") // Need valid validation template
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    validationPathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_{{.lang}}.tmpl")

    nonExistentResetPathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "non_existent_reset_{{.lang}}.tmpl")


    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "host_url": "http://host.com/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "languages": ["en"]
    }`, validationPathTemplate, nonExistentResetPathTemplate)

    expectedError := errors.New("read reset failed")

    // Mock functions
    mockResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        require.NoError(t, err)
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()

        if strings.Contains(actualPath, "non_existent_reset") {
            return nil, actualPath, expectedError // Simulate read error for reset template
        }
        // Assume validation template reads correctly
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        require.NoError(t, err, "Failed to read assumed valid template: "+actualPath)
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
         require.NoError(t, err)
         // Basic check to mimic isTemplateValid
        for _, name := range templateParts {
            if tmpl.Lookup(name) == nil {
                return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock template (valid) missing section %q", name)
            }
        }
        return tmpl, actualPath, err
    }
    mockValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }


    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError) // Error comes from validate.ReadTemplateFile(resetPathTempl, lang)
}

func TestValidatorInit_InvalidResetTemplateContent_303(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_en.tmpl") // Valid validation template
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    validationPathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_{{.lang}}.tmpl")


    invalidResetTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "invalid_reset_content_en.tmpl")
    invalidContent := `{{define "subject"}}S {{.Lang}}{{end}} {{define "body_plain"}}P {{.Lang}}{{end}}` // Missing html
    err = os.WriteFile(invalidResetTemplatePath, []byte(invalidContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    resetPathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "invalid_reset_content_{{.lang}}.tmpl")


    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "host_url": "http://host.com/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "languages": ["en"]
    }`, validationPathTemplate, resetPathTemplate)

    // Mock functions
    mockResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
         // Standard mock reader logic
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang})
        require.NoError(t, err)
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        if err != nil { return nil, actualPath, err}
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
         // Check validity for valid template file
         if strings.Contains(actualPath, "valid_en.tmpl") {
             require.NoError(t, err)
            for _, name := range templateParts {
                if tmpl.Lookup(name) == nil {
                    return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock template (valid) missing section %q", name)
                }
             }
         }
        // Return result for invalid template (potentially nil template and error)
        return tmpl, actualPath, err
    }
    mockValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
     // Error comes from isTemplateValid(v.resetTempl[idx])
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), fmt.Sprintf("parsing %s", invalidResetTemplatePath))
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), `template is missing section "body_html"`)
}

func TestValidatorInit_NoLanguages_404(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    configParts, err := minimalValidConfig(tempDir) // Provides default template path
    require.NoError(t, err)
    // No "languages" field added
    config := buildConfig(configParts)

    // Mock functions
    mockResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    // Expect ReadTemplateFile to be called with empty lang ""
    mockReadTemplateFile = mockGoodTemplateReader(t, "")
    mockValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Nil(t, validator.langMatcher) // No matcher should be created
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.validationTempl)
    assert.Len(t, validator.validationTempl, 1) // Should have one default template
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.resetTempl)
    assert.Len(t, validator.resetTempl, 1)      // Should have one default template
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.validationTempl[0])
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.resetTempl[0])
    // Check if templates were loaded correctly by looking for a known part
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.validationTempl[0].Lookup("subject"), "Default validation template missing subject")
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.resetTempl[0].Lookup("subject"), "Default reset template missing subject")
}

func TestValidatorInit_NoLangReadValidationError_505(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    nonExistentPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "no_such_file.tmpl")

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "https://example.com/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com"
    }`, nonExistentPath, nonExistentPath) // No languages, points to non-existent file

    expectedError := errors.New("read default failed")

    // Mock functions
    mockResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        assert.Empty(t, lang) // Expect empty lang for default
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"lang": lang}) // lang is ""
        require.NoError(t, err)
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        // Fail only on the first call (validation template)
        if actualPath == nonExistentPath {
             return nil, actualPath, expectedError
        }
        // Should not be reached if validation fails
        return nil, actualPath, errors.New("should not reach reset read")
    }
    mockValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError)

func TestValidatorInit_MissingSMTPHost_010(t *testing.T) {

    mockReadTemplateFile = mockGoodTemplateReader(t, "")
    // Mock ValidateHostURL to return "" for empty input, simulating missing/invalid URL
    mockValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) {
         if origUrl == "" { return "", nil} // Expected input when host_url is missing
         // Fail if a non-empty URL is somehow passed (shouldn't happen)
         return "", errors.New("unexpected host url")
    }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.EqualError(t, err, "missing SMTP host") // Error defined at line 197
}

Test Implementation:
func TestValidatorInit_DefaultsApplied_111(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    // Minimal config, relying on defaults and host derivation
    // host_url includes port and path to test hostname extraction
    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "http://my.cool-chat.app:8080/base/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.provider.net"
    }`, validTemplatePath, validTemplatePath) // Missing: max_retries, code_length, smtp_port, smtp_helo_host


    // Mock functions
    mockResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockReadTemplateFile = mockGoodTemplateReader(t, "") // Use default lang ""
    // Use a mock that mimics the real ValidateHostURL behavior needed here
    mockValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) {
         u, err := url.Parse(origUrl)
         if err != nil || u.Scheme == "" || u.Host == "" {
             return "", fmt.Errorf("invalid URL %s: %w", origUrl, err)
         }
         // Ensure trailing slash (as the original function does)
         if !strings.HasSuffix(origUrl, "/") {
             origUrl += "/"
         }
         return origUrl, nil
    }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Equal(t, defaultMaxRetries, validator.MaxRetries, "Default MaxRetries mismatch") // line 201
    assert.Equal(t, defaultCodeLength, validator.CodeLength, "Default CodeLength mismatch") // line 204
    assert.Equal(t, defaultPort, validator.SMTPPort, "Default SMTPPort mismatch")           // line 209
    // Check derived SMTPHeloHost (line 194)
    assert.Equal(t, "my.cool-chat.app", validator.SMTPHeloHost, "Derived SMTPHeloHost mismatch")
    // Check calculated maxCodeValue (line 206)
    expectedMaxCode := big.NewInt(0).Exp(big.NewInt(10), big.NewInt(int64(defaultCodeLength)), nil)
    assert.Equal(t, expectedMaxCode, validator.maxCodeValue, "Calculated maxCodeValue mismatch")
}

Required Library Installation
# No external libraries needed beyond testify and standard Go packages

Test Implementation:
func TestValidatorInit_ValidConfigLoginAuth_391(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t) // Setup mocking overrides and cleanup
    tempDir := t.TempDir()

    // Create template files for different languages
    validTemplatePathEn := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_template_en.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePathEn, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    validTemplatePathEs := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_template_es.tmpl")
    err = os.WriteFile(validTemplatePathEs, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    // Use template syntax in the config path (escaped for JSON)
    validationTemplatePathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_template_{{.Lang}}.tmpl")
    resetTemplatePathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_template_{{.Lang}}.tmpl") // Use same for simplicity

    validConfig := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "https://example.com/base/",
        "languages": ["en", "es"],
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "Test Sender <sender@example.com>",
        "login": "smtp-login",
        "sender_password": "smtp-password",
        "auth_mechanism": "login",
        "max_retries": 5,
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_port": "587",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "insecure_skip_verify": false,
        "domains": ["example.com"],
        "code_length": 8
    }`, jsonEscape(validationTemplatePathTemplate), jsonEscape(resetTemplatePathTemplate))

    // --- Mock validate package functions ---
    mockableResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil } // Pass path through

    mockableReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        // Execute the path template to get the actual path
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"Lang": lang}) // Use "Lang" as key like in template
        if err != nil {
            return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to execute path template for lang %s: %w", lang, err)
        }
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        t.Logf("Mock reading template for lang '%s' from path '%s'", lang, actualPath)

        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        if err != nil {
            return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock ReadFile failed for %s: %w", actualPath, err)
        }
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        if err != nil {
             return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock Parse failed for %s: %w", actualPath, err)
        }
        // Check validity
        for _, name := range templateParts {
            if tmpl.Lookup(name) == nil {
                 return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock template %s is missing section %q", actualPath, name)
            }
        }
        return tmpl, actualPath, err
    }

    mockableValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) {
         // Simple validation and ensure trailing slash
         u, err := url.Parse(origUrl)
         if err != nil || u.Scheme == "" || u.Host == "" {
             return "", fmt.Errorf("invalid URL %s: %w", origUrl, err)
         }
         if !strings.HasSuffix(origUrl, "/") {
             origUrl += "/"
         }
         return origUrl, nil
     }
    // --- End Mock Definitions ---

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(validConfig)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Equal(t, "https://example.com/base/", validator.HostUrl) // Check trailing slash added
    assert.Equal(t, []string{"en", "es"}, validator.Languages)
    assert.Equal(t, validationTemplatePathTemplate, validator.ValidationTemplFile) // Stores the template path itself
    assert.Equal(t, resetTemplatePathTemplate, validator.ResetTemplFile)
    assert.Equal(t, "sender@example.com", validator.senderEmail)
    assert.Equal(t, "smtp-login", validator.Login)
    assert.Equal(t, "smtp-password", validator.SenderPassword)
    assert.Equal(t, "login", validator.AuthMechanism)
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.auth, "Auth should be configured")
    _, ok := validator.auth.(*loginAuth) // Check if it's the correct auth type
    assert.True(t, ok, "Expected auth to be of type *loginAuth")
    assert.Equal(t, 5, validator.MaxRetries)
    assert.Equal(t, "smtp.example.com", validator.SMTPAddr)
    assert.Equal(t, "587", validator.SMTPPort)
    assert.Equal(t, "mail.example.com", validator.SMTPHeloHost)
    assert.False(t, validator.TLSInsecureSkipVerify)
    assert.Equal(t, []string{"example.com"}, validator.Domains)
    assert.Equal(t, 8, validator.CodeLength)
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.validationTempl)
    assert.Len(t, validator.validationTempl, 2, "Should load templates for 2 languages")
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.resetTempl)
    assert.Len(t, validator.resetTempl, 2, "Should load templates for 2 languages")
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.langMatcher)
    assert.Equal(t, "100000000", validator.maxCodeValue.String()) // 10^8
}

func TestValidatorInit_AuthCramMD5_198(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    configParts, err := minimalValidConfig(t, tempDir)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    configParts["login"] = `"smtp-login"`
    configParts["sender_password"] = `"smtp-password"`
    configParts["auth_mechanism"] = `"cram-md5"`
    cramMd5Config := buildConfig(configParts)

    // Mock functions needed for successful init up to auth mechanism check
    mockableResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockableReadTemplateFile = mockGoodTemplateReader(t, "") // Use default lang "" for minimal config
    mockableValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(cramMd5Config)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.NotNil(t, validator.auth)
    // We can't easily check the exact type of smtp.CRAMMD5Auth's result,
    // but we can check that auth is not nil and the mechanism string is correct.
    assert.Equal(t, "cram-md5", validator.AuthMechanism)
    // Check it's not loginAuth (PlainAuth returns smtp.Auth, harder to distinguish)
    _, isLogin := validator.auth.(*loginAuth)
    assert.False(t, isLogin, "Auth should be CRAM-MD5, not login")
}

func TestValidatorInit_ResolveResetPathError_556(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir() // Need temp dir for the *valid* validation template
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_validation.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "/invalid/path/reset.tmpl"
    }`, jsonEscape(validTemplatePath))

    expectedError := errors.New("cannot resolve reset path")

    // Mock ResolveTemplatePath
    mockableResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) {
        if path == "/invalid/path/reset.tmpl" {
            return "", expectedError
        }
        // Assume validation path resolves correctly
        if path == validTemplatePath {
            return validTemplatePath, nil
        }
        return "", fmt.Errorf("unexpected path resolution call: %s", path)
    }
    // No need to mock ReadTemplateFile etc.

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError)
}

func TestValidatorInit_ParseValidationPathTemplateError_667(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    // This template path is invalid because of the unclosed action {{.Lang
    invalidPathTemplateConfig := `{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "validation_templ": "/path/to/{{.Lang",
        "reset_secret_templ": "/path/to/reset_{{.Lang}}.tmpl"
    }`

    // Mock ResolveTemplatePath to return the path as is

    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "unclosed action")        // Error type
}
