Test Implementation:
func TestValidatorInit_ReadValidationTemplateError_990(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    // Use a path template that points to a non-existent file for validation
    nonExistentTemplatePathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "non_existent_val_{{.Lang}}.tmpl")
    // Need a valid *reset* template path and file
    validResetTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "reset_en.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validResetTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    resetPathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "reset_{{.Lang}}.tmpl") // Path template for reset


    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "host_url": "http://host.com/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "languages": ["en"]
    }`, jsonEscape(nonExistentTemplatePathTemplate), jsonEscape(resetPathTemplate))

    expectedError := errors.New("read validation failed")

    // Mock functions
    mockableResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockableReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"Lang": lang})
        require.NoError(t, err)
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()

        // Simulate error only for validation template read attempt (based on expected non-existent path)
        if strings.Contains(actualPath, "non_existent_val") {
             return nil, actualPath, expectedError // Simulate read error
        }
        // Handle reset template read successfully
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        require.NoError(t, err, "Failed reading supposedly valid reset template: "+actualPath)
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        require.NoError(t, err)
        // Basic validation check for reset template
        for _, name := range templateParts {
            if tmpl.Lookup(name) == nil {
                return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock reset template %s missing %s", actualPath, name)
            }
        }
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    mockableValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    // Error comes from validate.ReadTemplateFile(validationPathTempl, lang)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError)
}

# No external libraries needed beyond testify and standard Go packages

Test Implementation:
func TestValidatorInit_InvalidValidationTemplateContent_101(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    invalidTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "invalid_content_en.tmpl")
    // Missing 'body_html'
    invalidContent := `{{define "subject"}}S {{.Lang}}{{end}} {{define "body_plain"}}P {{.Lang}}{{end}}`
    err := os.WriteFile(invalidTemplatePath, []byte(invalidContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    // Path template pointing to the invalid file (via execution)
    validationPathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "invalid_content_{{.Lang}}.tmpl")

    resetTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "reset_en.tmpl") // Valid reset template file
    err = os.WriteFile(resetTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    // Path template pointing to the valid reset file (via execution)
    resetPathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "reset_{{.Lang}}.tmpl")


    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "host_url": "http://host.com/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "languages": ["en"]
    }`, jsonEscape(validationPathTemplate), jsonEscape(resetPathTemplate))

    // Mock functions
    mockableResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockableReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        // Standard mock reader logic, will read invalid or valid content
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"Lang": lang})
        require.NoError(t, err)
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        if err != nil {
            // This might happen if the test setup is wrong, but shouldn't for this scenario
            return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mockReadTemplateFile failed to read %s: %w", actualPath, err)
        }
        // Parse the content. It might be invalid. Let Init handle the isTemplateValid check.
        tmpl, parseErr := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        // Return the parsed template (even if nil on error) and the parse error.
        // The Init function will then call isTemplateValid on the potentially valid template.
         if parseErr != nil {
             // If parsing itself fails, return that error
             return nil, actualPath, parseErr
         }
        // If parsing succeeded, return the template. isTemplateValid will be called later.
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    mockableValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }


    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    // Error comes from isTemplateValid(v.validationTempl[idx])
    // The path reported is the one returned by ReadTemplateFile
    expectedPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "invalid_content_en.tmpl")
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), fmt.Sprintf("parsing %s", expectedPath))
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), `template is missing section "body_html"`)
}

func TestValidatorInit_ReadResetTemplateError_202(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_en.tmpl") // Need valid validation template file
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    validationPathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_{{.Lang}}.tmpl") // Path template for validation

    nonExistentResetPathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "non_existent_reset_{{.Lang}}.tmpl") // Points to non-existent reset file

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "host_url": "http://host.com/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "languages": ["en"]
    }`, jsonEscape(validationPathTemplate), jsonEscape(nonExistentResetPathTemplate))

    expectedError := errors.New("read reset failed")

    // Mock functions
    mockableResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockableReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"Lang": lang})
        require.NoError(t, err)
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()

        if strings.Contains(actualPath, "non_existent_reset") {
            return nil, actualPath, expectedError // Simulate read error for reset template
        }
        // Assume validation template reads correctly
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        require.NoError(t, err, "Failed to read assumed valid template: "+actualPath)
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        require.NoError(t, err)
        // Basic check to mimic isTemplateValid
        for _, name := range templateParts {
            if tmpl.Lookup(name) == nil {
                return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock template (valid) missing section %q", name)
            }
        }
        return tmpl, actualPath, err // Use nil error here
    }
    mockableValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }


    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError) // Error comes from validate.ReadTemplateFile(resetPathTempl, lang)
}

func TestValidatorInit_InvalidResetTemplateContent_303(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_en.tmpl") // Valid validation template file
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    validationPathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_{{.Lang}}.tmpl") // Path template for validation

    invalidResetTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "invalid_reset_content_en.tmpl") // Invalid reset file
    invalidContent := `{{define "subject"}}S {{.Lang}}{{end}} {{define "body_plain"}}P {{.Lang}}{{end}}` // Missing html
    err = os.WriteFile(invalidResetTemplatePath, []byte(invalidContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    resetPathTemplate := filepath.Join(tempDir, "invalid_reset_content_{{.Lang}}.tmpl") // Path template for invalid reset


    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com",
        "host_url": "http://host.com/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "languages": ["en"]
    }`, jsonEscape(validationPathTemplate), jsonEscape(resetPathTemplate))

    // Mock functions
    mockableResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockableReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
         // Standard mock reader logic
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{"Lang": lang})
        require.NoError(t, err)
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        if err != nil { return nil, actualPath, err}
        tmpl, parseErr := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        if parseErr != nil {
             return nil, actualPath, parseErr
        }
         // Check validity for valid template file (validation template)
         if strings.Contains(actualPath, "valid_en.tmpl") {
             for _, name := range templateParts {
                if tmpl.Lookup(name) == nil {
                    return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock template (valid) missing section %q", name)
                }
             }
         }
        // Return potentially invalid reset template for isTemplateValid check
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    mockableValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
     // Error comes from isTemplateValid(v.resetTempl[idx])
    expectedPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "invalid_reset_content_en.tmpl")
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), fmt.Sprintf("parsing %s", expectedPath))
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), `template is missing section "body_html"`)
}

Test Implementation:
func TestValidatorInit_NoLangReadValidationError_505(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    nonExistentPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "no_such_file.tmpl")
    // Need valid path for reset template even though it shouldn't be reached
    validResetPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "reset_default.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validResetPath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)


    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "https://example.com/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com"
    }`, jsonEscape(nonExistentPath), jsonEscape(validResetPath)) // No languages

    expectedError := errors.New("read default validation failed")

    // Mock functions
    mockableResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockableReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        assert.Empty(t, lang, "Expected empty lang for default template read")
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        // Execute with empty lang map or suitable default context
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{})
        require.NoError(t, err)
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
         // If execution results in empty, maybe template name *was* the path
         if actualPath == "" && pathTempl.Name() != "" {
              actualPath = pathTempl.Name()
         }
         require.NotEmpty(t, actualPath, "Could not determine actual template path in mock reader")


        // Fail only on the first call (validation template) based on path
        if actualPath == nonExistentPath {
             return nil, actualPath, expectedError
        }
        // Handle successful read for reset template
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        require.NoError(t, err)
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        require.NoError(t, err)
         for _, name := range templateParts { // Basic validation
            if tmpl.Lookup(name) == nil { return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock reset tmpl missing %s", name)}
        }
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    mockableValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError)
}

Required Library Installation

Test Implementation:
func TestValidatorInit_NoLangInvalidValidationContent_606(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    invalidTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "invalid_default_val.tmpl")
    invalidContent := `{{define "subject"}}S{{end}}` // Missing body parts
    err := os.WriteFile(invalidTemplatePath, []byte(invalidContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    resetPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "reset_default.tmpl") // Valid reset template file
    err = os.WriteFile(resetPath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "https://example.com/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com"
    }`, jsonEscape(invalidTemplatePath), jsonEscape(resetPath)) // No languages

    // Mock functions
    mockableResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockableReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        assert.Empty(t, lang)
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{})
        require.NoError(t, err)
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        // If execution results in empty, maybe template name *was* the path
         if actualPath == "" && pathTempl.Name() != "" {
              actualPath = pathTempl.Name()
         }
         require.NotEmpty(t, actualPath, "Could not determine actual template path in mock reader")

        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        if err != nil { return nil, actualPath, err}
        tmpl, parseErr := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
         if parseErr != nil {
             // If parsing fails (e.g. syntax error in template), return that error
             return nil, actualPath, parseErr
         }
        // Return the template for isTemplateValid check
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    mockableValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), fmt.Sprintf("parsing %s", invalidTemplatePath))
    // Error comes from isTemplateValid check
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), `template is missing section "body_plain"`)
}

func TestValidatorInit_NoLangReadResetError_707(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_default_val.tmpl") // Need valid validation templ file
    err := os.WriteFile(validPath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    nonExistentResetPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "no_reset_default.tmpl") // Points to non-existent reset file

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "https://example.com/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com"
    }`, jsonEscape(validPath), jsonEscape(nonExistentResetPath)) // No languages

    expectedError := errors.New("read default reset failed")

    // Mock functions
    mockableResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockableReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        assert.Empty(t, lang)
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{})
        require.NoError(t, err)
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
        // If execution results in empty, maybe template name *was* the path
         if actualPath == "" && pathTempl.Name() != "" {
              actualPath = pathTempl.Name()
         }
         require.NotEmpty(t, actualPath, "Could not determine actual template path in mock reader")

        if actualPath == nonExistentResetPath {
             return nil, actualPath, expectedError // Fail on reset read
        }
        // Assume validation reads fine
        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        require.NoError(t, err)
        tmpl, err := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        require.NoError(t, err)
         // Basic check to mimic isTemplateValid
        for _, name := range templateParts {
            if tmpl.Lookup(name) == nil {
                return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock template (valid) missing section %q", name)
            }
        }
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    mockableValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.ErrorIs(t, err, expectedError)
}

func TestValidatorInit_NoLangInvalidResetContent_808(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_default_val.tmpl") // Valid validation templ file
    err := os.WriteFile(validPath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    invalidResetPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "invalid_reset_default.tmpl") // Invalid reset file
    invalidContent := `{{define "subject"}}S{{end}}` // Missing parts
    err = os.WriteFile(invalidResetPath, []byte(invalidContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "https://example.com/",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.example.com",
        "smtp_helo_host": "mail.example.com"
    }`, jsonEscape(validPath), jsonEscape(invalidResetPath)) // No languages

    // Mock functions
    mockableResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockableReadTemplateFile = func(pathTempl *textt.Template, lang string) (*textt.Template, string, error) {
        assert.Empty(t, lang)
        pathBuf := &bytes.Buffer{}
        err := pathTempl.Execute(pathBuf, map[string]string{})
        require.NoError(t, err)
        actualPath := pathBuf.String()
         // If execution results in empty, maybe template name *was* the path
         if actualPath == "" && pathTempl.Name() != "" {
              actualPath = pathTempl.Name()
         }
         require.NotEmpty(t, actualPath, "Could not determine actual template path in mock reader")

        content, err := os.ReadFile(actualPath)
        if err != nil { return nil, actualPath, err}
        tmpl, parseErr := textt.New(filepath.Base(actualPath)).Parse(string(content))
        if parseErr != nil { return nil, actualPath, parseErr }

         // Check validity for valid template file
         if actualPath == validPath {
            for _, name := range templateParts {
                if tmpl.Lookup(name) == nil {
                    return nil, actualPath, fmt.Errorf("mock template (valid) missing section %q", name)
                }
             }
         }
        // Return potentially invalid template for isTemplateValid check
        return tmpl, actualPath, nil
    }
    mockableValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) { return origUrl, nil }


    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.Error(t, err)
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), fmt.Sprintf("parsing %s", invalidResetPath))
    assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), `template is missing section "body_plain"`)
}

# No external libraries needed beyond testify and standard Go packages

Test Implementation:
func TestValidatorInit_DefaultsApplied_111(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    setupValidateMocks(t)
    tempDir := t.TempDir()
    validTemplatePath := filepath.Join(tempDir, "valid_default.tmpl")
    err := os.WriteFile(validTemplatePath, []byte(validTemplateContent), 0644)
    require.NoError(t, err)

    // Minimal config, relying on defaults and host derivation
    // host_url includes port and path to test hostname extraction
    hostURL := "http://my.cool-chat.app:8080/base/"
    config := fmt.Sprintf(`{
        "host_url": "%s",
        "validation_templ": "%s",
        "reset_secret_templ": "%s",
        "sender": "sender@example.com",
        "smtp_server": "smtp.provider.net"
    }`, hostURL, jsonEscape(validTemplatePath), jsonEscape(validTemplatePath)) // Missing: max_retries, code_length, smtp_port, smtp_helo_host

    // Mock functions
    mockableResolveTemplatePath = func(path string) (string, error) { return path, nil }
    mockableReadTemplateFile = mockGoodTemplateReader(t, "") // Use default lang ""
    // Use a mock that mimics the real ValidateHostURL behavior needed here
    mockableValidateHostURL = func(origUrl string) (string, error) {
         u, err := url.Parse(origUrl)
         if err != nil || u.Scheme == "" || u.Host == "" {
             return "", fmt.Errorf("invalid URL %s: %w", origUrl, err)
         }
         // Ensure trailing slash (as the original function does)
         validatedURL := origUrl
         if !strings.HasSuffix(validatedURL, "/") {
             validatedURL += "/"
         }
         return validatedURL, nil
    }

    validator := &validator{}

    // Act
    err = validator.Init(config)

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Equal(t, defaultMaxRetries, validator.MaxRetries, "Default MaxRetries mismatch") // line 201
    assert.Equal(t, defaultCodeLength, validator.CodeLength, "Default CodeLength mismatch") // line 204
    assert.Equal(t, defaultPort, validator.SMTPPort, "Default SMTPPort mismatch")           // line 209
    // Check derived SMTPHeloHost (line 194)
    assert.Equal(t, "my.cool-chat.app", validator.SMTPHeloHost, "Derived SMTPHeloHost mismatch")
    // Check calculated maxCodeValue (line 206)
    expectedMaxCode := big.NewInt(0).Exp(big.NewInt(10), big.NewInt(int64(defaultCodeLength)), nil)
    assert.Equal(t, expectedMaxCode.String(), validator.maxCodeValue.String(), "Calculated maxCodeValue mismatch")
}

func TestReplyCreateUser_AuthSecretNotUnique_789(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    mockSession := &Session{
        sid:         "session789",
        remoteAddr:  "127.0.0.1",
    }
    mockMsg := &ClientComMessage{
        Id:        "msg789",
        Timestamp: time.Now(),
        Acc: &MsgClientAcc{
            Secret: []byte("non_unique_secret"),
        },
    }

    // Mock the queueOut function
    mockSession.queueOut = func(msg *ServerComMessage) {
        assert.Equal(t, decodeStoreError(errors.New("auth secret not unique"), mockMsg.Id, mockMsg.Timestamp, map[string]any{"what": "auth"}), msg)
    }

    // Mock the store to return a mock auth handler
    mockAuthHandler := new(auth.MockAuthHandler)
    mockAuthHandler.On("IsUnique", mockMsg.Acc.Secret, mockSession.remoteAddr).Return(false, errors.New("auth secret not unique"))
    store.Store.GetLogicalAuthHandler = func(scheme string) auth.AuthHandler {
        return mockAuthHandler
    }

    // Act
    replyCreateUser(mockSession, mockMsg, nil)

    // Assert
    mockAuthHandler.AssertExpectations(t)
    logs.Warn.Println("create user: auth secret is not compliant", "auth secret not unique", "sid=", mockSession.sid)
}

func TestListenAndServe_HTTPAndHTTPS_123(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    addr := ":8080"
    mux := http.NewServeMux()
    tlsConfig := &tls.Config{}
    stop := make(chan bool)

    globals.shuttingDown = false
    globals.tlsRedirectHTTP = ":8081"

    // Mock netListener
    mockNetListener := func(addr string) (net.Listener, error) {
        return net.Listen("tcp", addr)
    }
    netListener = mockNetListener

    // Mock logs
    logs.Info = log.New(os.Stdout, "INFO: ", log.LstdFlags)
    logs.Err = log.New(os.Stderr, "ERROR: ", log.LstdFlags)

    // Act
    go func() {
        err := listenAndServe(addr, mux, tlsConfig, stop)
        assert.NoError(t, err)
    }()

    // Simulate stop signal
    time.Sleep(1 * time.Second)
    stop <- true

    // Assert
    assert.True(t, globals.shuttingDown)

    mockTopic := &Topic{name: topicName}

    storedTopic, ok := hub.topics.Load(topicName)

    assert.Equal(t, 1, hub.numTopics)
}

    "sync"
    "testing"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
)

Test Implementation:
func TestHub_topicDel_DeleteTopic_003(t *testing.T) {
    hub := &Hub{
        topics: &sync.Map{},
    }

    // Mock topic
    topicName := "testTopic"
    mockTopic := &Topic{name: topicName}
    hub.topics.Store(topicName, mockTopic)
    hub.numTopics = 1

    // Delete topic
    hub.topicDel(topicName)

    // Verify topic absence
    _, ok := hub.topics.Load(topicName)
    assert.False(t, ok)
    assert.Equal(t, 0, hub.numTopics)
}

import (
    "sync"
    "testing"
    "time"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
)

Test Implementation:
func TestHub_run_ProcessJoinMessages_005(t *testing.T) {
    hub := &Hub{
        topics:   &sync.Map{},
        join:     make(chan *ClientComMessage, 1),
        routeCli: make(chan *ClientComMessage, 1),
    }

    // Mock ClientComMessage
    joinMessage := &ClientComMessage{RcptTo: "testTopic", Original: "originalTopic"}
    hub.join <- joinMessage

    // Run hub in a separate goroutine
    go func() {
        hub.run()
    }()

    // Wait for processing
    time.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)

    // Verify topic creation
    topic, ok := hub.topics.Load("testTopic")
    require.True(t, ok)
    assert.NotNil(t, topic)
    assert.Equal(t, "testTopic", topic.(*Topic).name)
    assert.Equal(t, "originalTopic", topic.(*Topic).xoriginal)
}

func TestListenAndServe_HTTPServer_001(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    addr := ":8080"
    mux := http.NewServeMux()
    tlsConfig := (*tls.Config)(nil) // No TLS configuration for HTTP
    stop := make(chan bool)

    globals.shuttingDown = false
    globals.tlsRedirectHTTP = ""

    // Mock netListener
    mockNetListener := func(addr string) (net.Listener, error) {
        return net.Listen("tcp", addr)
    }
    netListener = mockNetListener

    // Mock logs
    logs.Info = log.New(os.Stdout, "INFO: ", log.LstdFlags)
    logs.Err = log.New(os.Stderr, "ERROR: ", log.LstdFlags)

    // Act
    go func() {
        err := listenAndServe(addr, mux, tlsConfig, stop)
        assert.NoError(t, err)
    }()

    // Simulate stop signal
    time.Sleep(1 * time.Second)
    stop <- true

    // Assert
    assert.True(t, globals.shuttingDown)
}

--------------------Test Case--------------------
Import Statements:
import (
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/golang/mock/gomock"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	mock_store "github.com/tinode/chat/mocks/server/store"
	"github.com/tinode/chat/server/store"
)

Required Library Installation
go get github.com/golang/mock/gomock

Test Implementation:
func TestHub_newHub_Initialization_198(t *testing.T) {
    // Note: This test assumes statsRegister* functions don't panic and
    //       the 'sys' topic join message can be processed or buffered.
    //       It primarily checks the struct initialization.

    // Mock globals expected by newHub and run->topicInit
    origCluster := globals.cluster
    origStore := globals.store
    defer func() {
        globals.cluster = origCluster
        globals.store = origStore
    }()
    globals.cluster = nil // Assume no cluster for basic init test
    // Mock store to prevent panics in topicInit if it's called immediately
    mockCtrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer mockCtrl.Finish()
    globals.store = mock_store.NewMockStoreClient(mockCtrl)

    hub := newHub()
    // hub.run() is started in a goroutine, we need to allow it to start
    // and process the initial 'sys' join message or ensure channels are buffered enough.
    time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond) // Give run() goroutine time to start

    assert.NotNil(t, hub.topics)
    assert.NotNil(t, hub.routeCli)
    assert.NotNil(t, hub.routeSrv)
    assert.NotNil(t, hub.join)
    assert.NotNil(t, hub.unreg)
    assert.NotNil(t, hub.rehash)
    assert.NotNil(t, hub.meta)
    assert.NotNil(t, hub.userStatus)
    assert.NotNil(t, hub.shutdown)
    // Initial numTopics is 0, but the 'sys' join might increment it
    // depending on how quickly run() processes it.
    // Let's check if 'sys' topic exists instead of numTopics count.
    _, ok := hub.topics.Load("sys")
    assert.True(t, ok, "sys topic should be initialized")

    // Close the hub to stop the run goroutine
    done := make(chan bool)
    hub.shutdown <- done
    <-done
}

func TestHub_run_JoinNewTopic_NoCluster_472(t *testing.T) {
    // Mock globals
    origCluster := globals.cluster
    origStore := globals.store
    defer func() {
        globals.cluster = origCluster
        globals.store = origStore
    }()
    globals.cluster = nil // No cluster

    mockCtrl := gomock.NewController(t)
    defer mockCtrl.Finish()
    mockStore := mock_store.NewMockStoreClient(mockCtrl)
    globals.store = mockStore

    // Mock topicInit dependencies (assuming it reads topic/subs from store)
    // Adjust mocks based on actual topicInit implementation
    mockStore.EXPECT().TopicGet(gomock.Any()).Return(nil, types.ErrNotFound).AnyTimes() // Simulate topic not existing initially
    mockStore.EXPECT().TopicCreate(gomock.Any(), gomock.Any(), gomock.Any()).Return(nil).AnyTimes() // Simulate successful creation

    hub := newHubInternal(true) // Use internal helper to skip starting run
    defer closeHub(hub)

    // Start run in a goroutine
    go hub.run()

    // Send join message
    topicName := "newTopic123"
    joinMessage := &ClientComMessage{
        RcptTo:   topicName,
        Original: "original:" + topicName,
        sess:     &Session{sid: "sess1"}, // Minimal session
    }
    hub.join <- joinMessage

    // Wait for processing
    time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond)

    // Verify topic creation
    topic, ok := hub.topics.Load(topicName)
    require.True(t, ok, "Topic should be created")
    require.NotNil(t, topic)
    topicConcrete := topic.(*Topic)
    assert.Equal(t, topicName, topicConcrete.name)
    assert.Equal(t, "original:"+topicName, topicConcrete.xoriginal)
    assert.False(t, topicConcrete.isProxy)
    assert.Nil(t, topicConcrete.master) // No master channel without cluster
    assert.Nil(t, topicConcrete.proxy)  // No proxy channel without cluster
}

	assert.NotNil(t, hub.routeSrv, "Hub routeSrv channel should be initialized")
	assert.NotNil(t, hub.join, "Hub join channel should be initialized")

	assert.NotNil(t, hub.rehash, "Hub rehash channel should be initialized")
	assert.NotNil(t, hub.meta, "Hub meta channel should be initialized")
	assert.NotNil(t, hub.userStatus, "Hub userStatus channel should be initialized")
	assert.NotNil(t, hub.shutdown, "Hub shutdown channel should be initialized")
	assert.Zero(t, hub.numTopics, "Hub should start with zero topics initially (before sys join is processed)")

	// Assert that the 'sys' topic join message was sent
	// Need to potentially receive from join or check length carefully
	select {
	case joinMsg := <-hub.join:
		require.NotNil(t, joinMsg, "A message should be in the join channel")

	case <-time.After(100 * time.Millisecond): // Timeout if no message
		t.Fatal("Initial 'sys' topic join message not found in join channel")
	}

	// hub.shutdown <- done
	// <-done // Wait for shutdown confirmation if implemented

import (

// Mock Session for testing hub interactions
type MockSession struct {
    sid          string
    out          chan *ServerComMessage // Channel to capture replies
    inflightReqs *sync.WaitGroup
    userID       types.Uid
}

func (s *MockSession) queueOut(msg *ServerComMessage) {
    if s.out != nil {
       select {
       case s.out <- msg:
       default:
          // Non-blocking send, useful if the test doesn't always expect a reply
       }
    }
}

func newMockSession(sid string, userID types.Uid) *MockSession {
    return &MockSession{
       sid:          sid,
       out:          make(chan *ServerComMessage, 5), // Buffered channel
       inflightReqs: &sync.WaitGroup{},
       userID:       userID,
    }
}

// Shared setup for hub tests
func setupHubTest(t *testing.T) (*Hub, func()) {
	// Reset globals
	globals.cluster = nil

    // Temporarily replace topicInit with a version that signals completion
    initCalled := make(chan string, 1)
    originalTopicInit := topicInit
    topicInit = func(tp *Topic, pkt *ClientComMessage, h *Hub) {
        initCalled <- tp.name // Signal that init was called for this topic
    }


	hub := newHub()
    // Drain the initial sys message
	<-hub.join


    // Teardown function
    teardown := func() {
        // Restore original topicInit
        topicInit = originalTopicInit
        close(initCalled)

        // Optionally shutdown hub if necessary, though closing channels might be enough
        // depending on how run() handles closed channels. For simplicity, omit full shutdown.
        // Attempt clean shutdown
        // shutdownDone := make(chan bool)
        // hub.shutdown <- shutdownDone
        // select {
        // case <- shutdownDone:
        // case <- time.After(100 * time.Millisecond):
        //  t.Log("Hub shutdown timed out")
        // }
        // close(hub.join) // Close channels to potentially stop the run loop gracefully if designed so
        // close(hub.shutdown)

    }

	return hub, teardown
}


func TestHubRun_JoinNewTopic_NoCluster_234(t *testing.T) {
	hub, teardown := setupHubTest(t)
	defer teardown()

	// Arrange
	topicName := "newTopicNoCluster123"
	sess := newMockSession("sess-1", types.ParseUserId("usr111"))
	joinMsg := &ClientComMessage{
		RcptTo:   topicName,
		Original: topicName,
		sess:     sess, // Assuming ClientComMessage has a session field `sess *Session` (it does implicitly via processing)
	}

	// Act
	hub.join <- joinMsg

	// Assert - wait for processing
	time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond) // Allow run loop to process

	// Check if topic was created
	topic := hub.topicGet(topicName)
	require.NotNil(t, topic, "Topic should have been created")
	assert.Equal(t, topicName, topic.name, "Topic name should match")
	assert.Equal(t, topicName, topic.xoriginal, "Topic original name should match")
	assert.False(t, topic.isProxy, "Topic should not be a proxy when cluster is nil")
	assert.Nil(t, topic.master, "Topic master channel should be nil")
	assert.Nil(t, topic.proxy, "Topic proxy channel should be nil")
	assert.NotNil(t, topic.exit, "Topic exit channel should be initialized")
	assert.True(t, topic.paused.Load(), "Topic should be paused initially") // Check paused status

    // Check if topicInit was called (using the signalling mechanism)
    select {
    case calledTopic := <- initCalled:
         assert.Equal(t, topicName, calledTopic, "topicInit should be called for the new topic")
    case <- time.After(50 * time.Millisecond):
         t.Errorf("topicInit was not called for topic %s", topicName)
    }
}

func TestHubRun_JoinExistingActiveTopic_Success_333(t *testing.T) {
	hub, teardown := setupHubTest(t)
	defer teardown()

	// Arrange: Manually add an active topic
	topicName := "existingActiveTopic789"
	existingTopic := &Topic{
		name:      topicName,
		xoriginal: topicName,
		sessions:  make(map[*Session]perSessionData),
		clientMsg: make(chan *ClientComMessage, 192),
		serverMsg: make(chan *ServerComMessage, 64),
		reg:       make(chan *ClientComMessage, 1), // Small buffer, ensure space
		unreg:     make(chan *ClientComMessage, 256),
		meta:      make(chan *ClientComMessage, 64),
		perUser:   make(map[types.Uid]perUserData),
		exit:      make(chan *shutDown, 1),
	}
    existingTopic.markPaused(false) // Ensure topic is active
	hub.topicPut(topicName, existingTopic)

	sess := newMockSession("sess-3", types.ParseUserId("usr333"))
	joinMsg := &ClientComMessage{
		RcptTo:   topicName,
		Original: topicName,
		sess:     sess,
        Timestamp: types.TimeNow(), // Add timestamp
	}

	// Act
	hub.join <- joinMsg

	// Assert: Check if the message arrived in the topic's reg channel
	select {
	case msgFromReg := <-existingTopic.reg:
		require.NotNil(t, msgFromReg, "Message should be received on topic reg channel")
		assert.Same(t, joinMsg, msgFromReg, "Received message should be the same as sent")
	case <-time.After(100 * time.Millisecond):
		t.Fatal("Message not received on topic reg channel within timeout")
	}

    // Ensure no error reply was sent to the session
    select {
    case errReply := <- sess.out:
        t.Fatalf("Received unexpected error reply: %+v", errReply)
    default:
        // No reply, as expected
    }
}

import (
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"
    "net/http"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	"github.com/tinode/chat/server/store/types"

Test Implementation:


func TestHubRun_JoinExistingActiveTopic_QueueFull_812(t *testing.T) {
	hub, teardown := setupHubTest(t)
	defer teardown()

	// Arrange: Manually add an active topic with a full reg channel
	topicName := "existingFullTopicABC"
	existingTopic := &Topic{
		name:      topicName,
		xoriginal: topicName,
		sessions:  make(map[*Session]perSessionData),
		clientMsg: make(chan *ClientComMessage, 192),
		serverMsg: make(chan *ServerComMessage, 64),
		reg:       make(chan *ClientComMessage), // Unbuffered channel, immediately full if not read
		unreg:     make(chan *ClientComMessage, 256),
		meta:      make(chan *ClientComMessage, 64),
		perUser:   make(map[types.Uid]perUserData),
		exit:      make(chan *shutDown, 1),
	}
    existingTopic.markPaused(false) // Ensure topic is active
	hub.topicPut(topicName, existingTopic)

	// Attempt to fill the channel (not strictly necessary for unbuffered, but safer)
	// Note: Since it's unbuffered, it's already full. No need to send anything.

    sess := newMockSession("sess-4", types.ParseUserId("usr444"))
    sess.inflightReqs.Add(1) // Simulate an inflight request

	joinMsg := &ClientComMessage{
		Id:       "msg-queue-full",
		RcptTo:   topicName,
		Original: topicName,
		sess:     sess,
        Timestamp: types.TimeNow(),
	}


	// Act
	hub.join <- joinMsg

	// Assert: Check if an error reply was sent and inflightReqs was decremented
    var wgDone bool
    wgChan := make(chan bool)
    go func() {
        sess.inflightReqs.Wait() // Wait for Done() to be called
        wgDone = true
        close(wgChan)
    }()

	select {
	case reply := <-sess.out:
		require.NotNil(t, reply, "Should receive a reply on session out channel")
        require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl, "Reply should be a Ctrl message")
        assert.Equal(t, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, reply.Ctrl.Code, "Reply code should be 503 Service Unavailable")
        assert.Equal(t, joinMsg.Id, reply.Ctrl.Id, "Reply ID should match original message ID")
        assert.Equal(t, topicName, reply.Ctrl.Topic, "Reply topic should match")
	case <-time.After(200 * time.Millisecond): // Increased timeout
		t.Fatal("Did not receive error reply on session out channel within timeout")
	}

    // Check if WaitGroup was decremented
    select {
    case <- wgChan:
         assert.True(t, wgDone, "inflightReqs.Done() should have been called")
    case <- time.After(100 * time.Millisecond):
        t.Fatal("inflightReqs.Wait() timed out, Done() was likely not called")
    }


    // Ensure the message did NOT go into the reg channel
	select {

import (

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	"github.com/tinode/chat/server/store/types"

Test Implementation:


func TestHubRun_JoinExistingInactiveTopic_567(t *testing.T) {
	hub, teardown := setupHubTest(t)
	defer teardown()

	// Arrange: Manually add an inactive topic
	topicName := "existingInactiveTopicDEF"
	existingTopic := &Topic{
		name:      topicName,
		xoriginal: topicName,
		sessions:  make(map[*Session]perSessionData),
		clientMsg: make(chan *ClientComMessage, 192),
		serverMsg: make(chan *ServerComMessage, 64),
		reg:       make(chan *ClientComMessage, 1),
		unreg:     make(chan *ClientComMessage, 256),
		meta:      make(chan *ClientComMessage, 64),
		perUser:   make(map[types.Uid]perUserData),
		exit:      make(chan *shutDown, 1),
	}
    existingTopic.markPaused(true) // Mark topic as inactive (paused)
	hub.topicPut(topicName, existingTopic)

    sess := newMockSession("sess-5", types.ParseUserId("usr555"))
    sess.inflightReqs.Add(1) // Simulate an inflight request

	joinMsg := &ClientComMessage{
		Id:       "msg-inactive",
		RcptTo:   topicName,
		Original: topicName,
		sess:     sess,
        Timestamp: types.TimeNow(),
	}

	// Act
	hub.join <- joinMsg

	// Assert: Check if an error reply was sent and inflightReqs was decremented
    wgChan := make(chan bool)
    go func() {
        sess.inflightReqs.Wait() // Wait for Done() to be called
        close(wgChan)
    }()

	select {
	case reply := <-sess.out:
		require.NotNil(t, reply, "Should receive a reply on session out channel")
        require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl, "Reply should be a Ctrl message")
        assert.Equal(t, http.StatusLocked, reply.Ctrl.Code, "Reply code should be 423 Locked")
        assert.Equal(t, joinMsg.Id, reply.Ctrl.Id, "Reply ID should match original message ID")
        assert.Equal(t, topicName, reply.Ctrl.Topic, "Reply topic should match")
	case <-time.After(100 * time.Millisecond):
		t.Fatal("Did not receive error reply on session out channel within timeout")
	}

    // Check if WaitGroup was decremented
    select {
    case <- wgChan:
        // OK, Done() was called
    case <- time.After(100 * time.Millisecond):
        t.Fatal("inflightReqs.Wait() timed out, Done() was likely not called")
    }

    // Ensure the message did NOT go into the reg channel
	select {
	case <-existingTopic.reg:
		t.Fatal("Message should not have been received on topic reg channel")
	default:
         // OK - channel remained empty
	}
}

Import Statements:
import (
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"net/url"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
)

Test Implementation:
func TestGetAPIKey_Precedence_831(t *testing.T) {
	form := url.Values{}
	form.Add("apikey", "formkey")
	req := httptest.NewRequest("POST", "/?apikey=querykey", strings.NewReader(form.Encode()))
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded")
	req.Header.Set("X-Tinode-APIKey", "headerkey")
	cookie := &http.Cookie{Name: "apikey", Value: "cookiekey"}
	req.AddCookie(cookie)
	err := req.ParseForm() // Parse form for FormValue
	require.NoError(t, err)

	// Check precedence: Header > Query > Form > Cookie
	assert.Equal(t, "headerkey", getAPIKey(req), "Header should have highest precedence")

	req.Header.Del("X-Tinode-APIKey")
	assert.Equal(t, "querykey", getAPIKey(req), "Query should be next")

	req.URL.RawQuery = "" // Remove query param
	assert.Equal(t, "formkey", getAPIKey(req), "Form should be next")

	// Clear form values to avoid interference in next step
	req.Form = nil // Clear parsed form explicitly

	// Create a new request to cleanly test the cookie without form values present
	req = httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/", nil) // No form body, no query
	req.AddCookie(cookie)
	assert.Equal(t, "cookiekey", getAPIKey(req), "Cookie should be last")
}

Test Implementation:
func TestCacheControlHandler_Enabled_365(t *testing.T) {
	maxAge := 3600
	strMaxAge := strconv.Itoa(maxAge)
	handler := cacheControlHandler(maxAge, http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
	}))

	req := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/", nil)
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()
	handler.ServeHTTP(rr, req)

	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rr.Code)
	expectedHeader := "must-revalidate, public, max-age=" + strMaxAge
	assert.Equal(t, expectedHeader, rr.Header().Get("Cache-Control"))
}

func TestCacheControlHandler_Disabled_741(t *testing.T) {
	handler := cacheControlHandler(0, http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
	}))

	req := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/", nil)
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()
	handler.ServeHTTP(rr, req)

	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rr.Code)
	assert.Empty(t, rr.Header().Get("Cache-Control"))

	handler = cacheControlHandler(-1, http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
	}))
	rr = httptest.NewRecorder()
	handler.ServeHTTP(rr, req)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rr.Code)
	assert.Empty(t, rr.Header().Get("Cache-Control"))
}

--------------------Test Case--------------------
Import Statements:
import (
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
)

Test Implementation:
func TestServe404_021(t *testing.T) {
	req := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/notfound", nil)
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()
	serve404(rr, req)

	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, rr.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "application/json; charset=utf-8", rr.Header().Get("Content-Type"))

	var msg ServerComMessage // Use the locally defined struct for testing
	err := json.Unmarshal(rr.Body.Bytes(), &msg)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NotNil(t, msg.Ctrl)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, msg.Ctrl.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "not found", msg.Ctrl.Text)
	assert.WithinDuration(t, time.Now(), msg.Ctrl.Timestamp, 5*time.Second) // Check timestamp is recent

Test Implementation:
func TestHttpErrorHandler_StatusOK_388(t *testing.T) {
	baseHandler := http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		w.Header().Set("X-Original", "true")
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
		_, _ = w.Write([]byte("OK response"))
	})
	errorHandler := httpErrorHandler(baseHandler)

	req := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/", nil)
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()
	errorHandler.ServeHTTP(rr, req)

	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rr.Code)
	// Original headers should be preserved if written before WriteHeader
	assert.Equal(t, "true", rr.Header().Get("X-Original"))
	// Content-Type is NOT set to json because status < 400
	assert.NotEqual(t, "application/json", rr.Header().Get("Content-Type"))
	assert.Equal(t, "OK response", rr.Body.String())
}

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

Test Implementation:
func TestHttpErrorHandler_StatusBadRequest_519(t *testing.T) {
	baseHandler := http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		// Try setting a different content type first
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "text/plain")
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusBadRequest)
		// This write should be ignored by the wrapper and replaced
		_, _ = w.Write([]byte("Original bad request message"))
	})
	errorHandler := httpErrorHandler(baseHandler)

	req := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/bad", nil)
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()
	errorHandler.ServeHTTP(rr, req)

	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusBadRequest, rr.Code)
	// Content-Type should be forced to application/json
	assert.Equal(t, "application/json", rr.Header().Get("Content-Type"))

	// Body should be the JSON error message
	var msg ServerComMessage // Use the locally defined struct for testing
	err := json.Unmarshal(rr.Body.Bytes(), &msg)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NotNil(t, msg.Ctrl)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusBadRequest, msg.Ctrl.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusText(http.StatusBadRequest), msg.Ctrl.Text) // "Bad Request"
	assert.WithinDuration(t, time.Now(), msg.Ctrl.Timestamp, 5*time.Second)
}

import (

Test Implementation:
func TestErrorResponseWriter_Write_Direct_873(t *testing.T) {
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()
	ew := &errorResponseWriter{0, rr}

	// Status < 400
	ew.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
	n, err := ew.Write([]byte("hello"))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, 5, n)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, ew.status)
	assert.Equal(t, "hello", rr.Body.String())
	assert.NotEqual(t, "application/json", rr.Header().Get("Content-Type")) // Ensure content type not forced

	// Status >= 400
	rr = httptest.NewRecorder()
	ew = &errorResponseWriter{0, rr}
	ew.WriteHeader(http.StatusInternalServerError)
	n, err = ew.Write([]byte("some original error")) // This content should be ignored
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.True(t, n > 0) // Write should report success and bytes written based on the JSON replacement
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusInternalServerError, ew.status)
	assert.Equal(t, "application/json", rr.Header().Get("Content-Type")) // Ensure content type IS forced

	var msg ServerComMessage // Use the locally defined struct for testing
	err = json.Unmarshal(rr.Body.Bytes(), &msg)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NotNil(t, msg.Ctrl)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusInternalServerError, msg.Ctrl.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusText(http.StatusInternalServerError), msg.Ctrl.Text)
}

Test Implementation:
func TestSignalHandler_ReturnsChannel_287(t *testing.T) {
	// The primary testable aspect is that it returns a channel.
	// The internal goroutine listening for OS signals is hard to trigger reliably
	// in a unit test without sending actual signals.
	stopChan := signalHandler()
	assert.NotNil(t, stopChan, "signalHandler should return a non-nil channel")

	// Optional: Check if the channel is usable (can be closed or sent to)
	// This doesn't test signal handling itself, just the channel mechanics.
	closed := make(chan bool)
	go func() {
		// Attempt to read, should block until signal or close
		<-stopChan
		close(closed)
	}()

	select {
	case <-closed:
		t.Log("Stop channel was closed or received a value unexpectedly early")
	case <-time.After(50 * time.Millisecond):
		// Expected path: the goroutine inside signalHandler is blocked waiting for a signal
		// and our reading goroutine is blocked reading from stopChan.
		t.Log("Channel appears operational (blocked as expected)")
	}
}

import (

Test Implementation:
func TestListenAndServe_ShutdownPath_Normal_331(t *testing.T) {
	mockStore, mockClust, mockH, mockGrpc, cleanup := setupTestGlobals(t)
	defer cleanup()

	stopChan := make(chan bool, 1)
	httpDoneChan := make(chan bool) // This channel remains open in this test case

	// --- Mock Expectations ---
	mockStore.On("Shutdown").Return()
	mockClust.On("shutdown").Return()
	// Check if grpcServer is expected to be nil or not based on typical setup
	if globals.grpcServer != nil { // Assume it might be non-nil
		mockGrpc.On("Stop").Return()
	} else {
		// If it's expected to be nil, don't set expectation for Stop()
	}


	// --- Action ---
	shutdownComplete := make(chan error)
	// Run listenAndServe shutdown logic simulation in a goroutine.
	go func() {
		// Simulate the select block logic directly based on the source.
	Loop:
		for {
			select {
			case <-stopChan:
				globals.shuttingDown = true
				ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Second)
				// Simulate calling server.Shutdown(ctx) -> returns nil
				// logs.Info.Println("Simulating successful server shutdown...")
				time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond) // Simulate work

				globals.sessionStore.Shutdown()
				globals.cluster.shutdown()
				pluginsShutdown()
				if globals.grpcServer != nil {
					globals.grpcServer.Stop()
				}
				statsShutdown()

				// Hub shutdown simulation
				hubDone := make(chan bool, 1) // Channel hub writes to
				// Ensure the write to hub.shutdown doesn't block if nothing reads it in the mock
				go func() {
					globals.hub.shutdown <- hubDone // Send channel to hub
				}()
				// Simulate hub completing work and writing to the channel
				go func() {
					// Simulate hub internal logic sending completion signal
					hubDone <- true
				}()
				<-hubDone // Wait for hub completion signal

				usersShutdown()
				cancel() // Cancel context after use

                // Simulate the <-httpdone read after server shutdown completes
                // In real code, httpdone receives true when Serve/ServeTLS returns.
                // After graceful shutdown, Serve/ServeTLS returns ErrServerClosed.
                // The select loop waits for httpdone *after* Shutdown().
                // Let's assume httpdone will receive true here.
                go func(){ httpDoneChan <- true }() // Simulate server stopping
                <-httpDoneChan // Consume the signal as in line 110

				shutdownComplete <- nil // Signal completion
				break Loop

			case <-httpDoneChan:
				shutdownComplete <- errors.New("httpdone triggered unexpectedly")
				break Loop

			case <-time.After(5 * time.Second):
				shutdownComplete <- errors.New("test loop timed out")
				break Loop
			}
		}
	}()

	// Trigger the shutdown path
	stopChan <- true

	// --- Assert ---
	select {
	case err := <-shutdownComplete:
		require.NoError(t, err, "Shutdown sequence failed")
		mockStore.AssertExpectations(t)
		mockClust.AssertExpectations(t)
		if globals.grpcServer != nil {
             mockGrpc.AssertExpectations(t)
             assert.True(t, pluginsShutdownCalled, "pluginsShutdown should be called")
		     assert.True(t, statsShutdownCalled, "statsShutdown should be called")
             assert.True(t, usersShutdownCalled, "usersShutdown should be called")
        } else {
             mockGrpc.AssertNotCalled(t, "Stop")
             assert.True(t, pluginsShutdownCalled, "pluginsShutdown should be called")
		     assert.True(t, statsShutdownCalled, "statsShutdown should be called")
             assert.True(t, usersShutdownCalled, "usersShutdown should be called")
        }
		assert.True(t, globals.shuttingDown, "globals.shuttingDown should be true")
	case <-time.After(6 * time.Second): // Test timeout > internal timeout
		t.Fatal("Test timed out waiting for shutdown completion")
	}
}

func TestListenAndServe_HttpDonePath_457(t *testing.T) {
	_, _, _, _, cleanup := setupTestGlobals(t)
	defer cleanup()

	stopChan := make(chan bool)
	httpDoneChan := make(chan bool, 1) // Buffered to allow sending

	// --- Action ---
	completionChan := make(chan error)
	// Simulate the select loop
	go func() {
	Loop:
		for {
			select {
			case <-stopChan:
				completionChan <- errors.New("stopChan triggered unexpectedly")
				break Loop
			case <-httpDoneChan:
				// Correct path for this test
				completionChan <- nil
				break Loop
			case <-time.After(3 * time.Second):
				completionChan <- errors.New("test loop timed out")
				break Loop
			}
		}
	}()

	// Trigger the httpDone path
	httpDoneChan <- true

	// --- Assert ---
	select {
	case err := <-completionChan:
		require.NoError(t, err, "httpDone path failed")
		// No shutdown functions should be called in this path
		assert.False(t, pluginsShutdownCalled)
		assert.False(t, statsShutdownCalled)
		assert.False(t, usersShutdownCalled)
		assert.False(t, globals.shuttingDown) // Should not be set in this path
	case <-time.After(4 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("Test timed out waiting for completion")
	}
}

// Mock Session for testing
type mockSession struct {
	Session // Embed original Session to satisfy interface if needed, though methods are mocked
	uid       types.Uid
	sid       string
	remoteAddr net.Addr
	lang      string
	outq      chan *ServerComMessage
	onLoginFn func(id string, ts time.Time, rec *auth.Rec, missing []string) *ServerComMessage
}

func (s *mockSession) queueOut(msg *ServerComMessage) {
	s.outq <- msg
}

func (s *mockSession) onLogin(id string, ts time.Time, rec *auth.Rec, missing []string) *ServerComMessage {
	if s.onLoginFn != nil {
		return s.onLoginFn(id, ts, rec, missing)
	}
	// Default mock implementation
	resp := NoErr(id, "", ts)
	resp.Ctrl.Params = map[string]any{
		"user":    rec.Uid.UserId(),
		"authlvl": rec.AuthLevel.String(),
		"token":   "mock_token",
		"expires": time.Now().Add(time.Hour).UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
	}
	if len(missing) > 0 {
		resp.Ctrl.Params["missingCreds"] = missing
	}
	return resp
}

// Setup function for tests
func setupUserTest(t *testing.T) (*mockSession, *storeMocks.PersistentStorageInterface, *authMocks.AuthHandler, *storeMocks.UsersPersistenceInterface, *storeMocks.FilesPersistenceInterface, *validateMocks.Validator) {
	// Init globals (replace with actual setup if needed)
	globals.cluster = &Cluster{
        localnode: "testnode",
    } // Mock cluster to avoid nil pointer in decodeStoreError
	globals.immutableTagNS = map[string]struct{}{"restricted:": {}}
	globals.authValidators = map[auth.Level][]string{
		auth.LevelAuth: {"email"}, // Example: require email for LevelAuth
		auth.LevelRoot: {"root"},
	}

	mockCtrl := mock.NewController(t)

	mockStore := storeMocks.NewMockPersistentStorageInterface(mockCtrl)
	mockAuthHandler := authMocks.NewMockAuthHandler(mockCtrl)
	mockUserStore := storeMocks.NewMockUsersPersistenceInterface(mockCtrl)
	mockFileStore := storeMocks.NewMockFilesPersistenceInterface(mockCtrl)
	mockValidator := validateMocks.NewMockValidator(mockCtrl)

	// Assign mocks to the global store variable
	// Use Interface() to get the underlying interface type expected by store package
	store.Users = mockUserStore
	store.Files = mockFileStore
	store.Store = mockStore

	sess := &mockSession{
		sid:        "test-sid",
		remoteAddr: &net.IPAddr{IP: net.ParseIP("1.2.3.4")},
		lang:       "en-US",
		outq:       make(chan *ServerComMessage, 1), // Buffered channel
	}

	return sess, mockStore, mockAuthHandler, mockUserStore, mockFileStore, mockValidator
}

func TestReplyCreateUser_SuccessNoLogin_123(t *testing.T) {
	sess, mockStore, mockAuthHandler, mockUserStore, mockFileStore, mockValidator := setupUserTest(t)
	defer mockStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockAuthHandler.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockUserStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockFileStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockValidator.Controller().Finish()

	msg := &ClientComMessage{
		Id:        "msg1",
		Timestamp: time.Now(),
		Acc: &MsgClientAcc{
			Scheme: "basic",
			Secret: []byte("password"),
			Login:  false,
			Tags:   []string{"tag1", "tag2"},
			Desc: &MsgSetDesc{
				DefaultAcs: &MsgDefaultAcsMode{Auth: "RW", Anon: "R"},
				Public:     map[string]string{"fn": "Test User"},
				Private:    map[string]string{"comment": "private comment"},
			},
			Cred: []MsgCredClient{
				{Method: "email", Value: "test@example.com", Response: "ok"},
			},
		},
		Extra: &MsgClientExtra{
			Attachments: []string{"file1", "file2"},
		},
		AuthLvl: int(auth.LevelGuest), // Not root
	}
	rec := &auth.Rec{Uid: types.ParseUserId("usrABCDE"), AuthLevel: auth.LevelAuth, Tags: []string{"tag1"}} // Example record
	tmpToken := []byte("temp-auth-token")

	// --- Mock Expectations ---
	mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler(msg.Acc.Scheme).Return(mockAuthHandler)
	mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().IsUnique(msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).Return(true, nil)
	mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
	mockValidator.EXPECT().PreCheck("test@example.com", nil).Return("email:test@example.com", nil)
	mockUserStore.EXPECT().Create(mock.AnythingOfType("*types.User"), msg.Acc.Desc.Private).
		DoAndReturn(func(user *types.User, private interface{}) (*types.User, error) {
			// Simulate DB assigning UID and timestamps
			user.ObjHeader.SetUid(rec.Uid)
			user.ObjHeader.InitTimes()
			return user, nil
		})
	mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().AddRecord(mock.Anything, msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).
		Return(rec, nil) // Return the user's auth record
	mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler("token").Return(mockAuthHandler) // For temp token generation
    mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().GenSecret(mock.AnythingOfType("*auth.Rec")).Return(tmpToken, time.Now().Add(time.Hour), nil)
    // Mocking addCreds internal calls (assuming its logic)
    mockUserStore.EXPECT().UpsertCred(mock.Any()).Return(true, nil) // Credential created
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
    mockValidator.EXPECT().Request(rec.Uid, "test@example.com", sess.lang, "ok", tmpToken).Return(true, nil)
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
    mockValidator.EXPECT().Check(rec.Uid, "ok").Return("email:test@example.com", nil)
    mockUserStore.EXPECT().UpdateTags(rec.Uid, mock.Any(), nil, mock.Any()).Return([]string{"tag1"}, nil).Maybe() // Could be called if tags change

	mockFileStore.EXPECT().LinkAttachments(rec.Uid.UserId(), types.ZeroUid, msg.Extra.Attachments).Return(nil)

	// --- Act ---
	replyCreateUser(sess, msg, nil) // Initial rec is nil

	// --- Assert ---
	select {
	case reply := <-sess.outq:
		require.NotNil(t, reply)
		require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl)
		assert.Equal(t, 201, reply.Ctrl.Code)
		assert.Equal(t, "created", reply.Ctrl.Text)
		assert.Equal(t, msg.Id, reply.Ctrl.Id)
		require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl.Params)
		params := reply.Ctrl.Params.(map[string]any)
		assert.Equal(t, rec.Uid.UserId(), params["user"])
		assert.Equal(t, rec.AuthLevel.String(), params["authlvl"])

		// Check desc in params
		desc, ok := params["desc"].(*MsgTopicDesc)
		require.True(t, ok)
		assert.NotNil(t, desc.CreatedAt)
		assert.NotNil(t, desc.UpdatedAt)
		assert.Equal(t, "RWPA", desc.DefaultAcs.Auth) // RW + Approve
		assert.Equal(t, "RPA", desc.DefaultAcs.Anon)  // R + Approve
		assert.Equal(t, msg.Acc.Desc.Public, desc.Public)
		assert.Equal(t, msg.Acc.Desc.Private, desc.Private)

	case <-time.After(1 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("timed out waiting for response")
	}
}

func TestReplyCreateUser_ErrAlreadyAuth_456(t *testing.T) {
	// Use a real session instance partially, just need uid and queueOut
	sess := &mockSession{
		sid:        "test-sid",
		remoteAddr: &net.IPAddr{IP: net.ParseIP("1.2.3.4")},
		uid:        types.ParseUserId("usrABCDE"), // Indicate authenticated session
		outq:       make(chan *ServerComMessage, 1),
	}

	msg := &ClientComMessage{
		Id:        "msg1",
		Timestamp: time.Now(),
		Acc: &MsgClientAcc{
			Scheme: "basic",
			Secret: []byte("password"),
			Login:  true, // Requesting login while already authenticated
		},
	}

	// --- Act ---
	replyCreateUser(sess, msg, nil) // rec is nil initially

	// --- Assert ---
	select {
	case reply := <-sess.outq:
		require.NotNil(t, reply)
		require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl)
		assert.Equal(t, 409, reply.Ctrl.Code) // 409 Conflict
		assert.Equal(t, ErrAlreadyAuthenticated("", "", time.Time{}).Ctrl.Text, reply.Ctrl.Text)
		assert.Equal(t, msg.Id, reply.Ctrl.Id)
	case <-time.After(1 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("timed out waiting for response")
	}

	// Test with non-nil rec
    sess2 := &mockSession{
		sid:        "test-sid-2",
		remoteAddr: &net.IPAddr{IP: net.ParseIP("1.2.3.4")},
		// uid is zero, but rec is non-nil
		outq:       make(chan *ServerComMessage, 1),
	}
    rec := &auth.Rec{Uid: types.ParseUserId("usrFGHIJ")}
    replyCreateUser(sess2, msg, rec)

    select {
	case reply := <-sess2.outq:
		require.NotNil(t, reply)
		require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl)
		assert.Equal(t, 409, reply.Ctrl.Code) // 409 Conflict
		assert.Equal(t, ErrAlreadyAuthenticated("", "", time.Time{}).Ctrl.Text, reply.Ctrl.Text)
		assert.Equal(t, msg.Id, reply.Ctrl.Id)
	case <-time.After(1 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("timed out waiting for response")
	}
}

func TestReplyCreateUser_ErrSecretNotUnique_101(t *testing.T) {
	sess, mockStore, mockAuthHandler, _, _, _ := setupUserTest(t)
	defer mockStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockAuthHandler.Controller().Finish()

	msg := &ClientComMessage{
		Id:        "msg3",
		Timestamp: time.Now(),
		Acc: &MsgClientAcc{
			Scheme: "basic",
			Secret: []byte("non-unique-secret"),
		},
	}
    testErr := types.ErrDuplicate // Example error

	// --- Mock Expectations ---
	mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler(msg.Acc.Scheme).Return(mockAuthHandler)
	mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().IsUnique(msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).Return(false, testErr)

	// --- Act ---
	replyCreateUser(sess, msg, nil)

	// --- Assert ---
	select {
	case reply := <-sess.outq:
		require.NotNil(t, reply)
		require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl)
        // decodeStoreError maps ErrDuplicate to 409 Conflict
		assert.Equal(t, 409, reply.Ctrl.Code)
        assert.Contains(t, reply.Ctrl.Text, "conflict") // Check text content
		assert.Equal(t, msg.Id, reply.Ctrl.Id)
        require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl.Params)
        params := reply.Ctrl.Params.(map[string]any)
        assert.Equal(t, "auth", params["what"]) // Check param from decodeStoreError
	case <-time.After(1 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("timed out waiting for response")
	}
}

	// --- Assert ---

func TestReplyCreateUser_ErrUserCreate_170(t *testing.T) {
	sess, mockStore, mockAuthHandler, mockUserStore, _, mockValidator := setupUserTest(t)
	defer mockStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockAuthHandler.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockUserStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockValidator.Controller().Finish()

	msg := &ClientComMessage{
		Id:        "msg8",
		Timestamp: time.Now(),
		Acc: &MsgClientAcc{
			Scheme: "basic",
			Secret: []byte("password"),
			Cred: []MsgCredClient{
				{Method: "email", Value: "test@example.com"},
			},
		},
		AuthLvl: int(auth.LevelGuest),
	}
    testErr := errors.New("db create failed")

	// --- Mock Expectations ---
	mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler(msg.Acc.Scheme).Return(mockAuthHandler)
	mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().IsUnique(msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).Return(true, nil)
	mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
	mockValidator.EXPECT().PreCheck("test@example.com", nil).Return("email:test@example.com", nil)
	mockUserStore.EXPECT().Create(mock.AnythingOfType("*types.User"), nil).Return(nil, testErr) // User create fails

	// --- Act ---
	replyCreateUser(sess, msg, nil)

	// --- Assert ---
	select {
	case reply := <-sess.outq:
		require.NotNil(t, reply)
		require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl)
        // Should return 500 Unknown based on the code path
		assert.Equal(t, 500, reply.Ctrl.Code)
        assert.Equal(t, ErrUnknown("","" , time.Time{}).Ctrl.Text, reply.Ctrl.Text)
		assert.Equal(t, msg.Id, reply.Ctrl.Id)
	case <-time.After(1 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("timed out waiting for response")
	}
}

func TestReplyCreateUser_ErrAddAuth_DeleteUser_183(t *testing.T) {
	sess, mockStore, mockAuthHandler, mockUserStore, _, mockValidator := setupUserTest(t)
	defer mockStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockAuthHandler.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockUserStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockValidator.Controller().Finish()

	msg := &ClientComMessage{
		Id:        "msg9",
		Timestamp: time.Now(),
		Acc: &MsgClientAcc{
			Scheme: "basic",
			Secret: []byte("password"),
			Cred: []MsgCredClient{
				{Method: "email", Value: "test@example.com"},
			},
		},
		AuthLvl: int(auth.LevelGuest),
	}
    createdUser := &types.User{}
    createdUser.ObjHeader.SetUid(types.ParseUserId("usrXYZ12"))
    createdUser.ObjHeader.InitTimes()

    addAuthErr := types.ErrInternal // Example error type mapped by decodeStoreError
    deleteErr := errors.New("failed to delete incomplete user")

	// --- Mock Expectations ---
	mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler(msg.Acc.Scheme).Return(mockAuthHandler)
	mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().IsUnique(msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).Return(true, nil)
	mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
	mockValidator.EXPECT().PreCheck("test@example.com", nil).Return("email:test@example.com", nil)
	mockUserStore.EXPECT().Create(mock.AnythingOfType("*types.User"), nil).Return(createdUser, nil) // User create succeeds
	mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().AddRecord(mock.Anything, msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).Return(nil, addAuthErr) // Add auth fails
	mockUserStore.EXPECT().Delete(createdUser.Uid(), true).Return(deleteErr) // User delete ALSO fails (to test logging)

	// --- Act ---
	replyCreateUser(sess, msg, nil)

	// --- Assert ---
	select {
	case reply := <-sess.outq:
		require.NotNil(t, reply)
		require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl)
        // Should return 500 Internal Server Error from decodeStoreError
		assert.Equal(t, 500, reply.Ctrl.Code)
		assert.Contains(t, reply.Ctrl.Text, "internal server error")
		assert.Equal(t, msg.Id, reply.Ctrl.Id)
        // Check that logs contain the delete error message (implicitly tested by coverage)
	case <-time.After(1 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("timed out waiting for response")
	}
}

func TestReplyCreateUser_ErrMissingCreds_196(t *testing.T) {
	sess, mockStore, mockAuthHandler, mockUserStore, _, mockValidator := setupUserTest(t)
	defer mockStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockAuthHandler.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockUserStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockValidator.Controller().Finish()

    // Setup globals: require 'email' and 'phone' for LevelAuth
    globals.authValidators[auth.LevelAuth] = []string{"email", "phone"}

	msg := &ClientComMessage{
		Id:        "msg10",
		Timestamp: time.Now(),
		Acc: &MsgClientAcc{
			Scheme: "basic",
			Secret: []byte("password"),
			Cred: []MsgCredClient{
				{Method: "email", Value: "test@example.com"}, // Only provides email, missing phone
			},
		},
		AuthLvl: int(auth.LevelGuest),
	}
    createdUser := &types.User{}
    createdUserUid := types.ParseUserId("usrMNO45")
    createdUser.ObjHeader.SetUid(createdUserUid)
    createdUser.ObjHeader.InitTimes()
    // Auth record returned after AddRecord determines the required creds level
    authRec := &auth.Rec{Uid: createdUserUid, AuthLevel: auth.LevelAuth, Tags: []string{"initial"}}

	// --- Mock Expectations ---
	mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler(msg.Acc.Scheme).Return(mockAuthHandler)
	mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().IsUnique(msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).Return(true, nil)
	mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
	mockValidator.EXPECT().PreCheck("test@example.com", nil).Return("email:test@example.com", nil)
	mockUserStore.EXPECT().Create(mock.AnythingOfType("*types.User"), nil).Return(createdUser, nil)
	mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().AddRecord(mock.Anything, msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).Return(authRec, nil)
    // Missing credential check happens here
	mockUserStore.EXPECT().Delete(createdUser.Uid(), true).Return(nil) // User delete should be called

	// --- Act ---
	replyCreateUser(sess, msg, nil)

	// --- Assert ---
	select {
	case reply := <-sess.outq:
		require.NotNil(t, reply)
		require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl)
        // decodeStoreError(types.ErrPolicy) results in 403 Forbidden
		assert.Equal(t, 403, reply.Ctrl.Code)
		assert.Contains(t, reply.Ctrl.Text, "policy violation")
		assert.Equal(t, msg.Id, reply.Ctrl.Id)
        require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl.Params)
        params := reply.Ctrl.Params.(map[string]any)
        missingCreds, ok := params["creds"].([]string)
        require.True(t, ok)
        assert.ElementsMatch(t, []string{"phone"}, missingCreds) // Check missing cred param
	case <-time.After(1 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("timed out waiting for response")
	}
}

func TestReplyCreateUser_ErrAddCreds_209(t *testing.T) {
	sess, mockStore, mockAuthHandler, mockUserStore, _, mockValidator := setupUserTest(t)
	defer mockStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockAuthHandler.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockUserStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockValidator.Controller().Finish()

    // Ensure required creds are met by the message
    globals.authValidators[auth.LevelAuth] = []string{"email"}

	msg := &ClientComMessage{
		Id:        "msg11",
		Timestamp: time.Now(),
		Acc: &MsgClientAcc{
			Scheme: "basic",
			Secret: []byte("password"),
			Cred: []MsgCredClient{
				{Method: "email", Value: "test@example.com", Response: "ok"},
			},
		},
		AuthLvl: int(auth.LevelGuest),
	}
    createdUser := &types.User{}
    createdUserUid := types.ParseUserId("usrPQR67")
    createdUser.ObjHeader.SetUid(createdUserUid)
    createdUser.ObjHeader.InitTimes()
    authRec := &auth.Rec{Uid: createdUserUid, AuthLevel: auth.LevelAuth, Tags: []string{"initial"}}
    tmpToken := []byte("temp-auth-token")
    addCredsErr := types.ErrValidationFailed // Example error from addCreds

	// --- Mock Expectations ---
	mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler(msg.Acc.Scheme).Return(mockAuthHandler)
	mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().IsUnique(msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).Return(true, nil)
	mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
	mockValidator.EXPECT().PreCheck("test@example.com", nil).Return("email:test@example.com", nil)
	mockUserStore.EXPECT().Create(mock.AnythingOfType("*types.User"), nil).Return(createdUser, nil)
	mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().AddRecord(mock.Anything, msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).Return(authRec, nil)
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler("token").Return(mockAuthHandler) // For temp token generation
    mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().GenSecret(mock.AnythingOfType("*auth.Rec")).Return(tmpToken, time.Now().Add(time.Hour), nil)
    // Mock the internal calls of addCreds to simulate failure
    mockUserStore.EXPECT().UpsertCred(mock.Any()).Return(true, nil) // Credential created
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
    mockValidator.EXPECT().Request(authRec.Uid, "test@example.com", sess.lang, "ok", tmpToken).Return(true, addCredsErr) // Request fails

	mockUserStore.EXPECT().Delete(createdUser.Uid(), true).Return(nil) // User delete should be called

	// --- Act ---
	replyCreateUser(sess, msg, nil)

	// --- Assert ---
	select {
	case reply := <-sess.outq:
		require.NotNil(t, reply)
		require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl)
        // decodeStoreError(ErrValidationFailed) returns 403 Forbidden
		assert.Equal(t, 403, reply.Ctrl.Code)
		assert.Contains(t, reply.Ctrl.Text, "validation failed")
		assert.Equal(t, msg.Id, reply.Ctrl.Id)
	case <-time.After(1 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("timed out waiting for response")
	}
}
