func TestReplyCreateUser_SuccessWithLogin_222(t *testing.T) {
	sess, mockStore, mockAuthHandler, mockUserStore, mockFileStore, mockValidator := setupUserTest(t)
	defer mockStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockAuthHandler.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockUserStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockFileStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockValidator.Controller().Finish()

    globals.authValidators[auth.LevelAuth] = []string{"email"} // Ensure requirements met

	msg := &ClientComMessage{
		Id:        "msg12",
		Timestamp: time.Now(),
		Acc: &MsgClientAcc{
			Scheme: "basic",
			Secret: []byte("password"),
			Login:  true, // Request login
			Tags:   []string{"login-tag"},
			Desc: &MsgSetDesc{
				Public: map[string]string{"fn": "Login User"},
			},
			Cred: []MsgCredClient{
				{Method: "email", Value: "login@example.com", Response: "ok"},
			},
		},
		AuthLvl: int(auth.LevelGuest),
	}
	createdUserUid := types.ParseUserId("usrLOG1N")
    authRec := &auth.Rec{Uid: createdUserUid, AuthLevel: auth.LevelAuth, Tags: []string{"login-tag"}}
    tmpToken := []byte("temp-auth-token")
    loginResponse := NoErr("login-id", "", time.Now()) // Mock login response
    loginResponse.Ctrl.Params = map[string]any{"user": createdUserUid.UserId(), "token": "session_token"}

    // --- Mock Expectations ---
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler(msg.Acc.Scheme).Return(mockAuthHandler)
    mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().IsUnique(msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).Return(true, nil)
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
    mockValidator.EXPECT().PreCheck("login@example.com", nil).Return("email:login@example.com", nil)
    mockUserStore.EXPECT().Create(mock.AnythingOfType("*types.User"), nil).
        DoAndReturn(func(user *types.User, private interface{}) (*types.User, error) {
            user.ObjHeader.SetUid(authRec.Uid)
            user.ObjHeader.InitTimes()
            return user, nil
        })
    mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().AddRecord(mock.Anything, msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).
        Return(authRec, nil)
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler("token").Return(mockAuthHandler)
    mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().GenSecret(mock.AnythingOfType("*auth.Rec")).Return(tmpToken, time.Now().Add(time.Hour), nil)
    // Mock addCreds calls
    mockUserStore.EXPECT().UpsertCred(mock.Any()).Return(true, nil)
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
    mockValidator.EXPECT().Request(authRec.Uid, "login@example.com", sess.lang, "ok", tmpToken).Return(true, nil)
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
    mockValidator.EXPECT().Check(authRec.Uid, "ok").Return("email:login@example.com", nil)
    mockUserStore.EXPECT().UpdateTags(authRec.Uid, mock.Any(), nil, mock.Any()).Return(authRec.Tags, nil).Maybe()
    mockFileStore.EXPECT().LinkAttachments(mock.Any(), mock.Any(), mock.Any()).Return(nil).Maybe() // No attachments in msg

    // Mock the s.onLogin call
    sess.onLoginFn = func(id string, ts time.Time, rec *auth.Rec, missing []string) *ServerComMessage {
        assert.Equal(t, msg.Id, id)
        assert.Equal(t, authRec.Uid, rec.Uid)
        assert.Empty(t, missing, "Should be no missing creds")
        // Add the desc to the login response as replyCreateUser does
         loginResponse.Ctrl.Params.(map[string]any)["desc"] = &MsgTopicDesc{
            CreatedAt: &authRec.State.ObjHeader.CreatedAt, // Assuming state is set in AddRecord mock or Create mock
            UpdatedAt: &authRec.State.ObjHeader.UpdatedAt,
            DefaultAcs: &MsgDefaultAcsMode{
                Auth: (types.ModeCP2P | types.ModeApprove).String(),
                Anon: (types.ModeCP2P | types.ModeApprove).String(),
            },
            Public:  msg.Acc.Desc.Public,
            Private: nil, // Private from Desc is not included in login response's desc
        }
        return loginResponse
    }

	// --- Act ---
	replyCreateUser(sess, msg, nil)

	// --- Assert ---
	select {
	case reply := <-sess.outq:
		require.NotNil(t, reply)
		require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl)
        // Assert the response is the one returned by the mocked onLoginFn
        assert.Equal(t, loginResponse.Ctrl.Code, reply.Ctrl.Code)
        assert.Equal(t, loginResponse.Ctrl.Text, reply.Ctrl.Text)
        assert.Equal(t, loginResponse.Ctrl.Id, reply.Ctrl.Id) // ID might differ if onLogin uses its own
        require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl.Params)
        params := reply.Ctrl.Params.(map[string]any)
        assert.Equal(t, createdUserUid.UserId(), params["user"])
        assert.Equal(t, "session_token", params["token"])
         // Check desc added to params
        desc, ok := params["desc"].(*MsgTopicDesc)
        require.True(t, ok)
        assert.NotNil(t, desc.CreatedAt)
        assert.NotNil(t, desc.UpdatedAt)
        assert.Equal(t, msg.Acc.Desc.Public, desc.Public)
        assert.Nil(t, desc.Private) // Private should not be in login desc

	case <-time.After(1 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("timed out waiting for response")
	}
}

    mockUserStore.EXPECT().UpdateTags(authRec.Uid, mock.Any(), nil, mock.Any()).Return(authRec.Tags, nil).Maybe()

	mockFileStore.EXPECT().LinkAttachments(createdUserUid.UserId(), types.ZeroUid, msg.Extra.Attachments).Return(linkErr) // LinkAttachments fails

func TestReplyCreateUser_CustomAccess_248(t *testing.T) {
	sess, mockStore, mockAuthHandler, mockUserStore, mockFileStore, mockValidator := setupUserTest(t)
	defer mockStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockAuthHandler.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockUserStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockFileStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockValidator.Controller().Finish()

    globals.authValidators[auth.LevelAuth] = []string{"email"} // Ensure requirements met

	msg := &ClientComMessage{
		Id:        "msg14",
		Timestamp: time.Now(),
		Acc: &MsgClientAcc{
			Scheme: "basic",
			Secret: []byte("password"),
			Login:  false,
			Desc: &MsgSetDesc{
				DefaultAcs: &MsgDefaultAcsMode{Auth: "RWP", Anon: "R"}, // Custom access
				Public:     map[string]string{"fn": "Access User"},
			},
			Cred: []MsgCredClient{
				{Method: "email", Value: "access@example.com", Response: "ok"},
			},
		},
		AuthLvl: int(auth.LevelGuest),
	}
    createdUserUid := types.ParseUserId("usrACCSS")
    authRec := &auth.Rec{Uid: createdUserUid, AuthLevel: auth.LevelAuth, Tags: []string{}}
    tmpToken := []byte("temp-auth-token")

    // --- Mock Expectations (Success path) ---
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler(msg.Acc.Scheme).Return(mockAuthHandler)
    mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().IsUnique(msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).Return(true, nil)
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
    mockValidator.EXPECT().PreCheck("access@example.com", nil).Return("email:access@example.com", nil)
    mockUserStore.EXPECT().Create(mock.AnythingOfType("*types.User"), nil).
        DoAndReturn(func(user *types.User, private interface{}) (*types.User, error) {
            user.ObjHeader.SetUid(authRec.Uid)
            user.ObjHeader.InitTimes()
            // Check if access modes were set correctly before saving
            expectedAuth := types.ModeRead | types.ModeWrite | types.ModePresence | types.ModeApprove
            expectedAnon := types.ModeRead | types.ModeApprove
            assert.Equal(t, expectedAuth, user.Access.Auth)
            assert.Equal(t, expectedAnon, user.Access.Anon)
            return user, nil
        })
    mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().AddRecord(mock.Anything, msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).
        Return(authRec, nil)
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler("token").Return(mockAuthHandler)
    mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().GenSecret(mock.AnythingOfType("*auth.Rec")).Return(tmpToken, time.Now().Add(time.Hour), nil)
    // Mock addCreds calls
    mockUserStore.EXPECT().UpsertCred(mock.Any()).Return(true, nil)
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
    mockValidator.EXPECT().Request(authRec.Uid, "access@example.com", sess.lang, "ok", tmpToken).Return(true, nil)
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
    mockValidator.EXPECT().Check(authRec.Uid, "ok").Return("email:access@example.com", nil)
    mockUserStore.EXPECT().UpdateTags(authRec.Uid, mock.Any(), nil, mock.Any()).Return(authRec.Tags, nil).Maybe()
    mockFileStore.EXPECT().LinkAttachments(mock.Any(), mock.Any(), mock.Any()).Return(nil).Maybe()

	// --- Act ---
	replyCreateUser(sess, msg, nil)

	// --- Assert ---
	select {
	case reply := <-sess.outq:
		require.NotNil(t, reply)
		require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl)
		assert.Equal(t, 201, reply.Ctrl.Code)
		require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl.Params)
		params := reply.Ctrl.Params.(map[string]any)
		desc, ok := params["desc"].(*MsgTopicDesc)
		require.True(t, ok)
        // Assert the access modes reflected in the response
        expectedAuthStr := (types.ModeRead | types.ModeWrite | types.ModePresence | types.ModeApprove).String()
        expectedAnonStr := (types.ModeRead | types.ModeApprove).String()
        assert.Equal(t, expectedAuthStr, desc.DefaultAcs.Auth)
        assert.Equal(t, expectedAnonStr, desc.DefaultAcs.Anon)
        assert.Equal(t, msg.Acc.Desc.Public, desc.Public)
	case <-time.After(1 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("timed out waiting for response")
	}
}

func TestReplyCreateUser_SetStateByRoot_261(t *testing.T) {
	sess, mockStore, mockAuthHandler, mockUserStore, mockFileStore, mockValidator := setupUserTest(t)
	defer mockStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockAuthHandler.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockUserStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockFileStore.Controller().Finish()
	defer mockValidator.Controller().Finish()

    globals.authValidators[auth.LevelAuth] = []string{"email"} // Ensure requirements met

	testState := types.StateSync // A valid state other than default
	msg := &ClientComMessage{
		Id:        "msg15",
		Timestamp: time.Now(),
		Acc: &MsgClientAcc{
			Scheme: "basic",
			Secret: []byte("password"),
			State:  testState.String(), // Root sets state
			Login:  false,
			Cred: []MsgCredClient{
				{Method: "email", Value: "state@example.com", Response: "ok"},
			},
		},
		AuthLvl: int(auth.LevelRoot), // Sender IS root
	}
    createdUserUid := types.ParseUserId("usrSTATE")
    authRec := &auth.Rec{Uid: createdUserUid, AuthLevel: auth.LevelAuth, Tags: []string{}}
    tmpToken := []byte("temp-auth-token")

    // --- Mock Expectations (Success path) ---
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler(msg.Acc.Scheme).Return(mockAuthHandler)
    mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().IsUnique(msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).Return(true, nil)
    // State validation passes
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
    mockValidator.EXPECT().PreCheck("state@example.com", nil).Return("email:state@example.com", nil)
    mockUserStore.EXPECT().Create(mock.AnythingOfType("*types.User"), nil).
        DoAndReturn(func(user *types.User, private interface{}) (*types.User, error) {
            assert.Equal(t, testState, user.State) // Check state before saving
            user.ObjHeader.SetUid(authRec.Uid)
            user.ObjHeader.InitTimes()
            return user, nil
        })
    mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().AddRecord(mock.Anything, msg.Acc.Secret, sess.remoteAddr.String()).
        Return(authRec, nil)
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetLogicalAuthHandler("token").Return(mockAuthHandler)
    mockAuthHandler.EXPECT().GenSecret(mock.AnythingOfType("*auth.Rec")).Return(tmpToken, time.Now().Add(time.Hour), nil)
    // Mock addCreds calls
    mockUserStore.EXPECT().UpsertCred(mock.Any()).Return(true, nil)
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
    mockValidator.EXPECT().Request(authRec.Uid, "state@example.com", sess.lang, "ok", tmpToken).Return(true, nil)
    mockStore.EXPECT().GetValidator("email").Return(mockValidator)
    mockValidator.EXPECT().Check(authRec.Uid, "ok").Return("email:state@example.com", nil)
    mockUserStore.EXPECT().UpdateTags(authRec.Uid, mock.Any(), nil, mock.Any()).Return(authRec.Tags, nil).Maybe()
    mockFileStore.EXPECT().LinkAttachments(mock.Any(), mock.Any(), mock.Any()).Return(nil).Maybe()

	// --- Act ---
	replyCreateUser(sess, msg, nil)

	// --- Assert ---
	select {
	case reply := <-sess.outq:
		require.NotNil(t, reply)
		require.NotNil(t, reply.Ctrl)
		assert.Equal(t, 201, reply.Ctrl.Code)
		// Further assertions on the reply content if needed
	case <-time.After(1 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("timed out waiting for response")
	}
}
